\displaystyle\mathscr{B}_{1}(v_{1},k,\beta):=\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,\frac{\omega}{2}-\beta\right)-1}{\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,\frac{\omega}{2}-\beta\right)}\frac{f_{0}(v_{1})}{\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})-\beta-\frac{\omega}{2}},

\displaystyle\mathscr{B}_{2}(v_{1},k,\beta):=-\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\Delta_{-k}f_{0}(v_{1})}{\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(-k,\frac{\omega}{2}+\beta\right)(\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})-\beta-\frac{\omega}{2})}\left<\frac{f_{0}(v_{3})}{\Delta_{-k}E(v_{3})-\beta-\frac{\omega}{2}}\right>_{\!\!v_{3}},

\displaystyle\mathscr{B}_{3}(v_{1},k,\beta):=\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\Delta_{-k}f_{0}(v_{1})}{(\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})-\beta-\frac{\omega}{2})\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,\frac{\omega}{2}-\beta\right)\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(-k,\frac{\omega}{2}+\beta\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\left(\left<\frac{f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})}{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta-\frac{\omega}{2}}\right>_{\!\!v_{2}}-\left<\frac{f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})}{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\right>_{\!\!v_{2}}\right).

Note that that for any function
g
,
\Delta_{k}g(v_{1}-\hbar k)=-\Delta_{-k}g(v_{1})
and that
\tilde{\varepsilon}(k,\bar{\omega})=\overline{\tilde{\varepsilon}(k,{\omega})}
. The function
\beta\mapsto\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,\frac{\omega}{2}-\beta\right)^{-1}
is holomorphic and bounded (thanks to Proposition
2.9
) in the half plane
\{\beta\in\mathbb{C},\penalty\ \Im\beta<\Im\tfrac{\omega}{2}\}
. In the same way
\beta\mapsto\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(-k,\frac{\omega}{4}+\beta\right)^{-1}
is holomorphic in
\{\beta\in\mathbb{C},\beta,\penalty\ \Im\beta>-\Im\tfrac{\omega}{2}\}
. Using the residue Theorem, for any
R>\max(\Im\omega,4\hbar^{2}|k|^{2})
large enough,

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathscr{B}_{1}(k,v_{1},\beta)\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{\beta}}{2i\pi}=&-\frac{f_{0}(v_{1})(\tilde{\varepsilon}(k,\omega-\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1}))-1)}{\tilde{\varepsilon}(k,\omega-\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1}))}\\
&+\int_{]-\infty,-R]\sqcup[-R,(-1+i)R]\sqcup[(-1+i)R,(1+i)R]\sqcup[(1+i)R,R]\sqcup[R,\infty[}B_{1}(k,v_{1},\beta)\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{\beta}}{2i\pi}\end{split}

Using the estimation of Proposition
2.9
, the remaining integral is of order
O(R^{-1})
, and vanishes as
R\to\infty
. We can conclude that (using that (
2.37
) and that
\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})+\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})=\hbar k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)
)

\begin{split}\lim_{\begin{subarray}{c}\omega\to 0\\
\Im\omega>|\Re\omega|\end{subarray}}\int\big(\mathscr{B}_{1}(k,v_{1},\beta)-\mathscr{B}_{1}(-k,&v_{1}^{\prime},\beta)\big)\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\beta}{2\pi i}=f_{0}(v_{1})\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,i0-\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})\right)}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(-k,i0-\Delta_{k}E(v_{1}^{\prime})\right)}\right)\\
&=\frac{2if_{0}(v_{1})}{|\tilde{\varepsilon}(k,+i0-\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1}))|^{2}}\Im\left(1+\frac{{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}}(k)}{(2\pi)^{d}}\left<\frac{\Delta_{k}f(v_{2})}{\hbar k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)+i0}\right>_{v_{2}}\right)\\
&=-\frac{2\pi i{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}}(k)f_{0}(v_{1})}{|\tilde{\varepsilon}(k,+i0-\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1}))|^{2}}\left<\Delta_{k}f(v_{2})\delta_{\hbar k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-k)}\right>_{v_{2}}.\end{split}

In the last line, we used the Sokhotskii-Plemelj formula: in the sens of measure, one has

\Im\frac{1}{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})+i0}=-\pi\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}.

In the same way, as
\mathscr{B}_{2}(v_{1},k,\beta)
is holomorphic on the half plane
\{\beta\in\mathbb{C},\penalty\ \Im\beta<\Im\tfrac{\omega}{2}\}
, one has

\lim_{\begin{subarray}{c}\omega\to 0\\
\Im\omega>|\Re\omega|\end{subarray}}\int\big(\mathscr{B}_{2}(k,v^{\prime}_{1},\beta)-\mathscr{B}_{2}(-k,-v_{1}^{\prime},\beta)\big)\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\beta}{2\pi i}=0.

We treat now
\mathscr{B}_{3}
. Note that
\mathscr{B}_{3}(k,v_{1},\beta))-\mathscr{B}_{3}(k,v_{1}^{\prime},-\beta)
is equal to

\displaystyle\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)}{\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,\frac{\omega}{2}-\beta\right)\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(-k,\frac{\omega}{2}+\beta\right)}\Bigg(\frac{\Delta_{-k}f_{0}(v_{1})}{\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})-\beta-\frac{\omega}{2}}-\frac{\Delta_{-k}f_{0}(v_{1})}{\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})-\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\Bigg(\left<\frac{f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})}{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\right>_{v_{2}}-\left<\frac{f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})}{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta-\frac{\omega}{2}}\right>_{v_{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\langle\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\Delta_{-k}f_{0}(v_{1})f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})}{\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,\frac{\omega}{2}-\beta\right)\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(-k,\frac{\omega}{2}+\beta\right)}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})-\beta-\frac{\omega}{2}}-\frac{1}{\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})-\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\Bigg(\frac{1}{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}_{v_{2}}-\frac{1}{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta-\frac{\omega}{2}}\Bigg)\Bigg\rangle_{v_{2}}

Using Lemma
2.9
, we obtain the following bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}\Bigg|\left<\frac{f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})}{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta+\frac{\omega}{2}}\right>_{v_{2}}-\left<\frac{f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})}{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta-\frac{\omega}{2}}\right>_{v_{2}}\Bigg|&\leq\left<\frac{f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})|\omega|}{\left|(\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\beta)^{2}+\omega^{2}/4\right|}\right>\\
&\leq C\|\langle v\rangle^{2}\langle\nabla\rangle^{2}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\log\left(1+\frac{|\Re\omega|}{|\Im\omega|}\right)\end{split}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int\left(\mathscr{B}_{3}(v_{1},k,\beta)-\mathscr{B}_{3}(v_{1}^{\prime},-k)\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{\beta}\right|\leq C{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\|\langle v\rangle^{2}\langle\nabla\rangle^{2}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{|\Re\omega|}{|\Im\omega|}\right)^{2}.

We need now the following variant of the Sokhotskii-Plemelj formula :

Lemma 2.10
.

In the sens of distribution in
\mathscr{D}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}_{\beta}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}_{v_{2}})
, one have

(2.39)

\lim_{\begin{subarray}{c}\omega\to 0\\
\Im\omega>|\Re\omega|\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{1}{\beta+\omega}-\frac{1}{\beta-\omega}\right)\left(\frac{1}{k(v_{2}-v_{1})+\beta+\omega}-\frac{1}{k(v_{2}-v_{1})+\beta-\omega}\right)=\delta_{\beta}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})},

with bounds that involves only the
0
-th and
1
-st order derivatives.

We deduce, using the
\mathscr{C}^{1}
bound in Lemma
2.9
, that

\lim_{\begin{subarray}{c}\omega\to 0\\
\Im\omega>|\Re\omega|\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int\left(\mathscr{B}_{3}(v_{1},k,\beta)-\mathscr{B}_{3}(v_{1}^{\prime},-k,-\beta)\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{\beta}\\[-5.0pt]
=\frac{2\pi i{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\Delta_{-k}f_{0}(v_{1})}{|\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,i0-\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})\right)|^{2}}\left<f_{0}(v_{2}-k)\delta_{\hbar k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\right>_{v_{2}}

Finally,

\lim_{\begin{subarray}{c}\omega\to 0\\
\Im\omega>|\Re\omega|\end{subarray}}\left(\left<\Big(K_{2}+B-{\omega}\Big)^{-1}A(v_{1},v_{2},k)\right>_{v_{2}}-\left<\Big(K_{2}+B-\omega\Big)^{-1}A(v_{1}^{\prime},v_{2}^{\prime},-k)\right>_{v_{2}}\right)\\
=\frac{2\pi i{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)}{|\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,i0-\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})\right)|^{2}}\left<(f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})f_{0}(v_{1}^{\prime})-f_{0}(v_{1})f_{0}(v_{2}))\delta_{\hbar k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\right>_{v_{2}},

and is uniformly bounded by
C{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\|f\|^{2}
.

∎

We can now conclude the proof of Theorem
1
:

First we have the identity

Q^{\hbar}_{LB}(f)(v_{1}):=2\left<\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{\Delta_{k}E(v_{2})+\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})}}{\hbar|\tilde{\varepsilon}\left(k,i0-\Delta_{-k}E(v_{1})\right)|^{2}}(f_{0}(v_{2}^{\prime})f_{0}(v_{1}^{\prime})-f_{0}(v_{1})f_{0}(v_{2}))\right>_{v_{2},k}.

Using dominated convergence theorem,

\displaystyle{\mu}\int_{0}^{\infty}\!\left<\partial_{t}{f}_{\mu}(\tau_{N}t,v_{1})\varphi(t,v_{1})\right>_{\!\!v_{1}}\!\!\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}\underset{N\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{\alpha}\left<\frac{\tilde{\varphi}(\alpha,v_{1})}{1+i\alpha}Q_{LB}(f_{0})(v_{1})\right>_{v_{1}}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\left<{\varphi}(t,v_{1})Q^{\hbar}_{LB}(f_{0})(v_{1})\right>_{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}

One can now use the estimation of Propositions
2.3
and
2.5
. For
\varphi:\mathbb{R}_{t}^{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}_{v_{1}}
a smooth test function with compact support in time in
[0,T_{*}]
, and
\tau_{N}
such that
\tfrac{N\tau_{N}}{\hbar\mu}=(\log N)^{1-\alpha}
for some
\alpha\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle{\mu}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{(2\pi)^{d}}{L^{d}}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}_{L}}\varphi\left(t,\hbar{k}\right)\partial_{t}\hat{F}_{N}^{1}(\tau_{N}t,k,k)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}-{\mu}\int_{0}^{\infty}\!\left<\partial_{t}{f}_{\mu}(\tau_{N}t,v_{1})\varphi(t,v_{1})\right>_{\!\!v_{1}}\!\!\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}

\displaystyle=O\left(\|\mu\partial_{t}(\hat{F}_{N}^{1}-\hat{F}_{\mu,L})\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}],\mathscr{L}^{1})}\right)+O\left(\|\mu\partial_{t}({f}_{\mu}-{f}_{\mu,L})\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}],E_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(N^{-1/4}+\frac{\exp\left({C\frac{T_{*}\tau_{N}}{\hbar}}\right)}{L/\hbar}\right)

for some constant
C
. Fixing
L\geq 2\hbar\log\tfrac{\tau_{N}}{\hbar}
, the preceding expression converges to
0
.

3.
Bound of the dielectric constant
\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F,k,v_{1})

We begin by bounding the dielectric constant
\varepsilon_{\hbar}
. The bounds are similar to the one of
\varepsilon_{0}
provided in
[
DW23
]
.

Proposition 3.1
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
. For any
\mathscr{V}
satisfying Assumption
2
,

(1)

Non-degenerency at the Maxwellian
: For all
k,v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

(3.1)

|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v)|\simeq 1.

(2)

Stability at fix
\hbar
: for any
\Phi,\Phi^{\prime}
measurable

(3.2)

|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)-\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\tilde{\Phi},k,v)|\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}(\Phi-\tilde{\Phi})\|_{H^{2}}.

If there is
f,\tilde{f}
such that
\Phi=M+\sqrt{M}f
and
\tilde{\Phi}=M+\sqrt{M}\tilde{f}
,

(3.3)

|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)-\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\tilde{\Phi},k,v)|\lesssim\left\|f-\tilde{f}\right\|_{2}.

(3)

Limit when
\hbar\to 0
: for any
\Phi
measurable

(3.4)

|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)-\varepsilon_{0}(\Phi,k,v)|\lesssim\hbar\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}\Phi\right\|_{H^{3}}.

If there is
f
such that
\Phi=M+\sqrt{M}f
,

(3.5)

|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)-\varepsilon_{0}({\Phi},k,v)|\lesssim\hbar(1+\left\|f\right\|_{3}).

(4)

Boundedness: For
(r,q,\ell)\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
,
\ell\leq 1
, and for any
\Phi
measurable, denoting
\hat{k}:=\tfrac{k}{|k|}
,

(3.6)

|\nabla_{\hat{k}}^{r}\nabla_{v}^{q}\nabla_{|k|}^{\ell}\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)|\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{r}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+r}f\right\|_{H^{r+q+\ell+2}}

If there is
f
such that
\Phi=M+\sqrt{M}f
,

(3.7)

|\nabla_{\hat{k}}^{r}\nabla_{v}^{q}\nabla_{|k|}^{\ell}\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)|\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{r}\left(1+\left\|f\right\|_{r+q+\ell+2}\right)

Proof.

We will only prove the non-pertubativ forms of the estimations. The pertubativ forms can be deduced from

\forall\ell,r\in\mathbb{N}\|\langle v\rangle^{r}(\sqrt{M}f)\|_{H^{\ell}}\lesssim_{r,\ell}\|f\|_{\ell}.

Step
1.
The proof of (
1
) has been performed in Lemma 2.1 of
[
DW23
]
.

Step
2.
Proof of (
2
).
Setting
\hat{k}:=\tfrac{k}{|k|}
,

\begin{split}\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)&=1+{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)}\int_{-1}^{0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\int\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla\Phi(v_{*}+s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-v_{*}-\hbar k)-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}=1+{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)}\int_{1}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\int\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla\Phi(v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-v_{*})-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}\end{split}

Using Sobolev’s inequalities, we deduce for any
s\in[1,2]
,

\left\|\int\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla\Phi(v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-v_{*})-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\left\|\int\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla\Phi(v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-v_{*})-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}\hat{k})}

Splitting the integral over
v_{*}\in<\hat{k}>\oplus<\hat{k}>^{\perp}
, and applying the usual bound on the Hilbert transform and trace operator,

\begin{split}\left\|\int\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla\Phi(v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-v_{*})-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}&\lesssim\left\|\int\hat{k}\cdot\nabla\Phi(v_{*}^{\perp}+\cdot)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}^{\perp}}\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}\hat{k})}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}\Phi\|_{H^{2}}\end{split}

Step
3.
Proof of (
3
). We have

\varepsilon_{\hbar}(f,k,v)-\varepsilon_{0}({f},k,v)={{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)}\int_{1}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\int\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla\left[f(v_{*}-s\hbar k)-f(v_{*})\right]}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-v_{*})-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}.

Using the same estimation as Step 2,

|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(f,k,v)-\varepsilon_{0}({f},k,v)|\lesssim|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|\int_{1}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}\langle\nabla\rangle^{2}\left[f(\cdot-s\hbar k)-\Phi\right]\right\|_{L^{2}}\\
\lesssim\hbar\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}\Phi\right\|_{H^{3}}.

Step
4.
Proof of (
4
). As

\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)=1+{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(|k|)\int_{1}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\int\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla^{q}\nabla\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar|k|\hat{k})}{\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}

We begin by the derivatives in
v
and in
|k|
:

\displaystyle\nabla_{v}^{q}\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)={\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(|k|)\int_{1}^{2}\int\frac{{k}\cdot\nabla^{q+1}\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}

\displaystyle\nabla_{|k|}\nabla_{v}^{q}\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi,k,v)=\int_{1}^{2}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{\prime}(|k|)\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla^{q+1}\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}+\hbar|k|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(|k|)\frac{\hat{k}^{\otimes 2}:\nabla^{q+2}\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}.

Hence, without lost of generality, we can suppose that
\ell=q=0
.

We apply the Leibniz formula to

\nabla_{\hat{k}}^{r}\frac{\hat{k}\cdot\nabla^{q}\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}

Fix
r_{1},r_{2}\in\mathbb{N}
such that
r_{1}+r_{2}=r
. One has

\nabla_{\hat{k}}^{r_{1}}\hat{k}\cdot\nabla^{q+1}\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)=(-s\hbar)^{r_{1}}\hat{k}\cdot\nabla^{q+r_{1}+1}\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)+(-s\hbar)^{r_{1}-1}\nabla^{q+r}\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)

Denoting
v_{*}^{\shortparallel}:=(\hat{k}\cdot v_{*})\hat{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{\hat{k}}^{r_{2}}\frac{1}{\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r_{3}=0}^{r_{2}}\binom{r_{2}}{r_{3}}(-1)^{r_{2}}r_{2}!\frac{\hat{k}^{\otimes(r_{2}-r_{3})}\otimes_{\rm sym}(v_{*}^{\perp})^{\otimes r_{3}}}{(\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0)^{r_{3}+1}}

\displaystyle=r_{2}!\sum_{r_{3}=0}^{k}\binom{r_{2}}{r_{3}}\frac{(-1)^{r_{3}}}{(r_{2}-r_{3})!}\left[\hat{k}^{\otimes r3}:\nabla_{v_{*}}^{r_{3}}\frac{1}{\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}\right]{\hat{k}^{\otimes r_{2}-r_{3}}\otimes_{\rm sym}(v_{*}^{\perp})^{\otimes r_{3}}}

Using integration by part, one can transfer the
r_{3}
derivative in
v_{*}
into
F
.

\int\nabla_{v_{*}}^{r_{3}}\frac{1}{\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}(v_{*}^{\perp})^{\otimes r_{3}}\nabla^{q+r_{1}}\Phi(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}=\int\frac{(v_{*}^{\perp})^{\otimes r_{3}}\nabla^{q+r_{1}+r_{3}}f(v-v_{*}-s\hbar k)}{\hat{k}\cdot v_{*}-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}\\
=O\left(\langle v\rangle^{r}_{3}\left(1+\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+r_{3}}\Phi\right\|_{H^{q+r_{1}+r_{3}}}\right)\right)

Using that
{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}
respects Assumption
2
, we can conclude the proof.
∎

We deduce from the preceding lemma the following bound

Corollary 3.2
.

There exist two constants
C_{0}>0
and
\hbar_{0}>0
such that for any
\hbar<\hbar_{0}
and
\|f\|_{3}<C_{0}
, we have
|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M+\sqrt{M}f,k,v)|\simeq 1
.

4.
Pertubative solutions of the Quantum Lenard–Balescu equation

The next part is dedicated to the proof of Theorem
2
. The proof is based on the work of Duerinckx and Winter
[
DW23
]
, based on
[
Guo10
]
.

4.1.
The discrete difference norm

In order to control the linearized collision operator
\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}
, we need to introduce an adapted norm, which looks like a Sobolev norm with wait.

Definition 4.1
.

We introduce
\mathscr{H}_{\hbar}
the Hilbert space of norm
\|\penalty\ \|_{\hbar}
(with
\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}:=\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k
)

(4.1)

\|g\|_{\hbar}^{2}:=\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left[\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}M_{2}+g_{1}^{2}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}.

In the same way, we introduce for
\hbar=0
the space
\mathscr{H}_{0}
with norm

(4.2)

\displaystyle\|g\|^{2}_{0}:=\int\nabla g_{1}\left(\int B(v_{1},v_{2})M_{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\right)\nabla g_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+\int g_{1}v_{1}\left(\int B(v_{1},v_{2})M_{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\right)g_{1}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

(4.3)

\displaystyle B(v_{1},v_{2}):=B(M,v_{1},v_{2})=\int\frac{|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}k\otimes k}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}.

We begin with a general proposition:

Proposition 4.1
.

Fix
v_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
k_{0}>0
and
M_{0}>\tfrac{2}{k_{0}}
. Then, for any measurable function
g
, and any
s>0

(4.4)

\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{|\hbar k|\leq 1}\frac{(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{d+2+s}\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\\
\lesssim_{M_{0},v_{0},k_{0}}\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\begin{subarray}{c}M_{0}|\hat{k}\cdot(v_{1}-v_{0})|\leq 1\\
M_{0}||k|-k_{0}|\leq 1\end{subarray}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Proof.

Step
1.
We want to prove that

(4.5)

\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{|\hbar k|\leq 1}\frac{(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{d+2+s}\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\\
\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{M_{0}|k|\leq 1}\frac{(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Fix
R\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
. For
k\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
\tfrac{R}{2M_{0}}\leq|k|<\tfrac{R+1}{2M_{0}}
, Cauchy-Schwartz inequality gives

\displaystyle\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{d+2+s}}\lesssim\frac{1}{R^{d+2+s}}\sum_{j=0}^{R-1}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar(j+1)\frac{k}{R})-g(v_{1}+\hbar j\frac{k}{R}))^{2}}{|\frac{k}{R}|}

We deduce that for
R\leq\tfrac{1}{\hbar}

\displaystyle\int_{|\hbar k|\leq 1}\frac{(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{d+3}\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{R-1}\int_{\tfrac{R}{2M_{0}}\leq|k|<\tfrac{R+1}{2M_{0}}}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar(j+1)\frac{k}{R})-g(v_{1}+\hbar j\frac{k}{R}))^{2}}{R^{d+2+s}|\frac{k}{R}|\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{R^{1+s}}\int_{\tfrac{1}{2M_{0}}\leq|k|<\tfrac{1}{M_{0}}}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}}{|k|\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Summing with respect to
R\in\mathbb{N}\cap[0,\hbar^{-1}]
, we deduce Estimation (
4.5
)

Step
2.
We want to prove that

(4.6)

\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{M_{0}|k|\leq 1}\frac{(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\lesssim M_{0}^{3}\langle v_{0}\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{M_{0}|k|\leq 1}\frac{(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|}\mathbbm{1}_{M_{0}|\hat{k}\cdot(v_{1}-v_{0})|\leq 1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Performing the change of variable
v_{1}\mapsto v_{1}+v_{0}
and that
\langle v_{1}\rangle\geq\langle v_{1}+v_{0}\rangle/\langle v_{0}\rangle
, we can suppose that
v_{0}=0
.

We introduce the change of variable
k\mapsto(|k|,x,\sigma)\in\mathbb{R}^{+}\times[0,1]\times\mathbb{S}^{d-2}_{v_{1}^{\perp}}
, (where we denote
\mathbb{S}^{d-2}_{v^{\perp}_{1}}:=\{\sigma\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}|\sigma\cdot v_{1}=0\}
) defined by

(4.7)

k=|k|\left(\sqrt{1-x^{2}}\sigma+x\tfrac{v_{1}}{|v_{1}|}\right),\penalty\ |\sigma|=1=0,\text{ and }\sigma\cdot v_{1}=0.

The Jacobian of this transformation is

\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k=\frac{|k|^{d-1}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{d-3}{2}}}{(2\pi)^{d}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{\sigma}.

We suppose now that
|k|<M_{0}
and that
\tfrac{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|}{3M_{0}}\in]R-1,R]
for some
R\in\mathbb{N}\setminus\{0,1\}
. We define
k^{R}_{1},k^{R}_{2}
by

\displaystyle k^{R}_{1}:=\frac{|k|}{2}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{R\sqrt{1-{x^{2}}/{R^{2}}}}\right)\left(\sqrt{1-{x^{2}}/{R^{2}}}\sigma+\frac{x}{R}\hat{v_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle k^{R}_{2}:=\frac{|k|}{2}\left(1-\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{R\sqrt{1-{x^{2}}/{R^{2}}}}\right)\left(-\sqrt{1-{x^{2}}/{R^{2}}}\sigma+\frac{x}{R}\hat{v_{1}}\right).

One has
k^{R}_{1}+k^{R}_{2}=\tfrac{1}{R}k
and
\tfrac{|k_{1}^{R}|}{|k|},\tfrac{|k_{2}^{R}|}{|k|}\in[\frac{1}{4},1]
. In addition, the Jacobian of the application
k\mapsto k_{i}^{R}
is

\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k_{i}^{R}=\frac{1}{R}\left(\frac{1}{2}-(-1)^{i}\frac{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}{2R\sqrt{1-{x^{2}}/{R^{2}}}}\right)^{d-1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k=:\frac{1}{RJ_{i}^{R}(k)}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k,

where
J_{i}^{R}(k)\in[\tfrac{1}{4^{d-1}},1]
.

We perform the decomposition

(4.8)

(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}\\
\leq R\sum_{j=0}^{R-1}\big(g(v_{1}+\hbar(\tfrac{j}{R}k+k_{1}^{R}+k_{2}^{R}))-g(v_{1}+\hbar(\tfrac{j}{R}k+k_{1}^{R})\big)^{2}+\big(g(v_{1}+\hbar(\tfrac{j}{R}k+k_{1}^{R}))-g(v_{1}+\hbar\tfrac{j}{R}k)\big)^{2}

Note that for any
j\in[0,R]
, we have

\hbar\big|\tfrac{j}{R}k\big|\leq\hbar|k|\leq\tfrac{\hbar}{2M_{0}}\text{, and }\hbar\big|jRk+k_{1}^{R}\big|\leq\sqrt{2}|\hbar k|\leq\tfrac{\sqrt{2}\hbar}{2M_{0}}.

Then, denoting
\Omega_{R}:=\{|k|<\tfrac{1}{2M_{0}},\penalty\ \tfrac{R-1}{3M_{0}}<{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|}\leq\tfrac{R}{3M_{0}}\}
and performing the changes of variable
v_{1}\mapsto v_{1}+j(k_{1}^{R}+k_{2}^{R})
and
v_{1}\mapsto v_{1}+j(k_{1}^{R}+k_{2}^{R})+k_{1}^{R}
, for
\hbar<(6M_{0})^{-2}
,

\int_{\Omega_{R}}\frac{(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\lesssim\frac{M_{0}^{3}}{R^{5/2}}\int_{|k|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\left[(g(v_{1}+\hbar k_{1}^{R})-g(v_{1}))^{2}+(g(v_{1}+\hbar k_{2}^{R})-g(v_{1}))^{2}\right]\\
\times\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|\in\left[\frac{R-1}{6M_{0}},\frac{R}{2M_{0}}\right]}}{|k|}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Using the change of variable
k\mapsto k_{1}^{R}
,
k\mapsto k_{2}^{R}
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{R}}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\lesssim\frac{M_{0}^{3}}{R^{3/2}}\int_{|k|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{|k|}(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Finally,

\int_{|k|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k=\sum_{R=1}^{\infty}\int_{\Omega_{R}}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}(g_{1}^{\prime}-g_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\\
\lesssim\sum_{R=1}^{\infty}\frac{M_{0}^{3}}{R^{3/2}}\int_{|k|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{|k|}(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

This conclude the proof of (
4.6
).

Step
3.
We need to change the integration set of
k
: denoting
k=|k|\hat{k}
and
k^{\prime}=|k^{\prime}|\hat{k}
,

\displaystyle\int_{2M_{0}|k|\leq 1}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}}{|k|}\mathbbm{1}_{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\lesssim M_{0}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}2M_{0}|k|\leq 1\\
2M_{0}||k^{\prime}|-k_{0}|\leq 1\end{subarray}}\left[(g(v_{1}+\hbar k^{\prime})-g(v_{1}))^{2}+(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}+\hbar k^{\prime}))^{2}\right]\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}}{|k|}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}|k^{\prime}|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim M_{0}\int_{2M_{0}|k|\leq 1}|k|^{d-2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}|k|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}M_{0}||k^{\prime}|-k_{0}|\leq 1\end{subarray}}(g(v_{1}+\hbar k^{\prime})-g(v_{1}))^{2}\tfrac{|k^{\prime}|^{d-1}}{k_{0}^{d-1}}\mathbbm{1}_{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}|k^{\prime}|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\hat{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{M_{0}^{d}}{k_{0}^{d-1}}\int_{2M_{0}|k|\leq 1}\tfrac{|k|^{d-2}}{\langle k\rangle^{d}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}|k|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}M_{0}||k|-k_{0}|\leq 1\end{subarray}}(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|\leq\frac{1}{2M_{0}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}|k|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\hat{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}

This conclude the proof.
∎

Corollary 4.2
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
.

There exists
\hbar_{0}>0
such that for any
\hbar\in(0,\hbar_{0})
,
\alpha\in(0,1/2]
and
g
a test function,

(4.9)

\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\alpha(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-{1/2}}g\|^{2}\simeq\|g\|_{\hbar}.

where the constant are independent of
\hbar
.

Remark 4.1
.

It has been proved in
[
DW23
]
that

(4.10)

\|g\|_{0}^{2}\simeq\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\tfrac{v}{|v|}\cdot\nabla g(v)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left({\rm Id}-\tfrac{v\otimes v}{|v|^{2}}\right)\nabla g(v)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}g(v)\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

Step
1.
We want to prove the upper bound

(4.11)

\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int g_{1}^{2}\left({M_{2}}^{\alpha}-{M^{\prime}_{2}}^{\alpha}\right)^{2}{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{-{1/2}}g\|^{2}

First, for
\alpha\in(0,\tfrac{1}{2})
,

\int g_{1}^{2}\left({M_{2}}^{\alpha}-{M^{\prime}_{2}}^{\alpha}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}=\int{g_{1}}^{2}\frac{C{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{\hbar^{2}|k|}\left(e^{-\alpha(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+\hbar k)^{2}}-e^{-\alpha(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}\right)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

We use the change of variable introduce the change of variable
k\mapsto(|k|,x,\sigma)\in\mathbb{R}^{+}\times[0,1]\times\mathbb{S}^{d-2}_{v_{1}}
, introduce in (
4.7
).

(4.12)

\displaystyle\int

\displaystyle\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}\left(e^{-\alpha(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+\hbar k)^{2}}-e^{-\alpha(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}\right)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

\displaystyle=\int\tfrac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d-2}}{(2\pi)^{d}}\left(\int_{0}^{1}2\alpha\hbar k(|v_{1}|x+s\hbar|k|)\exp\left(-\alpha(x|v_{1}|+s\hbar|k|)^{2}\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\right)^{2}(1-x^{2})^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}

\displaystyle\lesssim\hbar^{2}\alpha^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}\int_{-1}^{1}\exp\left(-\alpha(x|v_{1}|+s\hbar|k|)^{2}\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\lesssim\frac{\hbar^{2}\alpha^{3/2}}{<v_{1}>}.

Step
2.
We treat the lower bound

(4.13)

\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int g_{1}^{2}\left({M_{2}}^{\alpha}-{M^{\prime}_{2}}^{\alpha}\right)^{2}{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\gtrsim\|\langle v\rangle^{-{3/2}}|v|g\|^{2}-C\hbar^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{-{1/2}}g\|^{2}

for some constant
C>0
. We can restrict our self to the
k
that verifies
|k|<1
.

\int_{-1}^{1}\left(\int_{0}^{1}(|v_{1}|x+s\hbar|k|)e^{-\alpha(|v_{1}|x+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s\right)^{2}(1-x^{2})^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\\
\begin{split}&\gtrsim\int_{-1/2}^{1/2}\left(|v_{1}x|^{2}-\frac{\hbar^{2}|k|}{4}\right)e^{-2\alpha\left(|v_{1}x|^{2}+|\hbar k|^{2}\right)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}x\\
&\gtrsim\frac{C}{|v_{1}|}\int_{-2|v_{1}|}^{2|v_{1}|}y^{2}e^{-2\alpha y^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}y-O\left(\frac{\hbar^{2}}{\langle v_{1}\rangle}\right)\geq\frac{C_{\alpha}|v_{1}|}{\langle v_{1}\rangle^{3}}-O\left(\frac{|k|^{2}\hbar^{2}}{\langle v_{1}\rangle}\right).\end{split}

We can conclude by integrating with respect to
k
.

Step
3.
We treat now the "differential part" of the norm: for
\alpha\in(0,1)
, there exits a constant
C>0
such that

\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)({M^{\prime}_{2}})^{2\alpha}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}=\frac{C}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{\left(v_{1}\cdot{\hat{k}}\right)^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}.

We begin by cutting the large
k
and
v_{1}
. Using Assumption
2
, we have

\displaystyle\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}\exp\left(-\frac{\alpha\left(v_{1}\cdot{k}\right)^{2}}{|k|^{2}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{|k|\geq\frac{1}{\hbar}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\frac{1}{\hbar}}^{\infty}\int_{-1}^{1}{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}|k|^{d-2}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{d-3}{2}}e^{-\alpha|v_{1}|^{2}x^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}|k|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\hbar^{4}}{\langle v_{1}\rangle}.

As
{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}
respects Assumption
2
, there exists
k_{0},M_{0},C_{0}>0
such that
{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}>\tfrac{1}{C_{0}}\mathbbm{1}_{|k-k_{0}|>\frac{1}{M_{0}}}
. Hence, applying Proposition
4.1
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{|\hbar k|\leq 1}\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{\left(v_{1}\cdot{\hat{k}}\right)^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\begin{subarray}{c}M_{0}|k_{0}-|k||\leq 1\\
|\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}|\leq 1\end{subarray}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\alpha\left(v_{1}\cdot{\hat{k}}\right)^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}.

Step
4.
We prove the integrability near
0
:

\int_{|v_{1}|<1}g(v_{1})^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\leq C\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}2<|k|<3\\
|v_{1}|<1\end{subarray}}(g(v_{1}+k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}2<|k|<3\\
|v_{1}|<1\end{subarray}}(g(v_{1}+k))^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

Using the convex inequality, one can bound
\int_{|v_{1}|<1}g(v_{1})^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}
by (for some
C>0
)

\displaystyle C\left\lfloor\hbar^{-1}\right\rfloor\sum_{n=0}^{\left\lfloor\hbar^{-1}\right\rfloor-1}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}2<|k|<3\\
|v_{1}|<1\end{subarray}}\left(g\left(v_{1}+\frac{(n+1)}{\left\lfloor\hbar^{-1}\right\rfloor}k\right)-g\left(v_{1}+\frac{n}{\left\lfloor\hbar^{-1}\right\rfloor}k\right)\right)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}+C\|\langle v\rangle^{-3/2}|v|g\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}2<|k|<3\\
|v_{1}|<1+3\hbar\end{subarray}}\frac{\left(g\left(v_{1}+\frac{k}{\left\lfloor\hbar^{-1}\right\rfloor}\right)-g(v_{1})\right)^{2}}{\left\lfloor\hbar^{-1}\right\rfloor^{-2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}+C\|\langle v\rangle^{-3/2}|v|g\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left(v_{1}\cdot{\hat{k}}\right)^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+C\|\langle v\rangle^{-3/2}|v|g\|^{2},

where we use the change of variable
k\mapsto\tfrac{\hbar^{-1}}{\lfloor\hbar^{-1}\rfloor}k
and that
2<|k|<3
,
|v_{1}|<4
.

Hence

\|\langle v\rangle^{-1/2}g\|^{2}\lesssim\int\frac{\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left(v_{1}\cdot{\hat{k}}\right)^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+C\|\langle v\rangle^{-3/2}|v|g\|^{2}

Step
5.
Conclusion

Using Step 1, for any
\alpha<\tfrac{1}{2}
,

\|g\|_{\hbar}^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-2\alpha(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1/2}g\|^{2}.

Using Steps 2, 3 and 4, for any
\alpha<\tfrac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\alpha(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1/2}g\|^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{1}}-{g^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\tfrac{1}{2}(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\|g\|_{\hbar}

\displaystyle+\|\langle v\rangle^{-3/2}|v|g\|^{2}+\hbar^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{-1/2}g\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|g\|_{\hbar}+\hbar^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{-1/2}g\|^{2}.

As the constant does not depend on
\hbar
, the estimation (
4.9
) holds for
\hbar\leq\hbar_{0}
small enough.
∎

Proposition 4.3
.

There exists a constant
C
such that for any test function
g\in L^{2}
,

(4.14)

0\leq\hbar<\hbar^{\prime}\Rightarrow\|g\|_{\hbar^{\prime}}\leq C\|g\|_{\hbar}.

Proof.

We recall that

\|g\|^{2}_{\hbar}\simeq\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g(v_{1})}-{g(v_{1}+\hbar k)}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{-1/2}g\|^{2}

Hence we only need to focus on the first terms.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g(v_{1})}-{g(v_{1}+\hbar k)}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left[\left({g(v_{1})}-{g(v_{1}+\frac{\hbar}{2}k)}\right)^{2}+\left(g(v_{1}+\frac{\hbar}{2}k)-{g(v_{1}+\hbar k)}\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{(\hbar/2)^{2}}\int\left({g(v_{1})}-{g(v_{1}+\frac{\hbar}{2}k)}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

For
q\in]\frac{1}{2},1]
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g(v_{1})}-{g(v_{1}+\hbar k)}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\left(v_{1}\cdot{\hat{k}}\right)^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}=

\displaystyle\frac{q^{2}}{(q\hbar)^{2}}\int\left({g(v_{1})}-{g(v_{1}+q\hbar k)}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(qk)}{|qk|}e^{-\left(v_{1}\cdot{\hat{k}}\right)^{2}}\frac{\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}}{q^{d}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g(v_{1})}-{g(v_{1}+q\hbar k)}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\left(v_{1}\cdot{\hat{k}}\right)^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

Finally we use that for any
0<\hbar<\hbar^{\prime}
, there exists a couple
(r,q)\in\mathbb{N}\times]1/2,1]
such that
\hbar=q2^{-r}\hbar^{\prime}
.

The case
\hbar=0
can be deduce by taking the limit for smooth test functions.
∎

4.2.
Decomposition of the quadratic form
\langle g,\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}g\rangle

We can decompose
\langle g,\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}g\rangle
into three peaces:

\displaystyle\int g\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}g=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\hbar^{2}}\int\left(g_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}+g_{2}\sqrt{M_{1}}-g^{\prime}_{1}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}-g^{\prime}_{2}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

(4.15)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left[\left(g_{1}-g^{\prime}_{1}\right)^{2}M_{2}+g_{1}^{\prime 2}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

(4.16)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left[\left(g_{1}-g^{\prime}_{1}\right)g_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

(4.17)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left(g_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-g^{\prime}_{1}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)\left(g_{2}\sqrt{M_{1}}-g^{\prime}_{2}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

This decomposition will be necessary in the proof of Proposition
4.6
, which is the crucial step of the proof. One can identify (
4.15
) with
\|g\|_{\hbar}^{2}/2
. The goal of this section is to study the remaining parts.

We need to introduce a cutoff function.

Definition 4.2
.

Fix
\chi:\mathbb{R_{+}}\to[0,1]
an increasing function, with
\chi(1)=1
,
\chi(0)=0
and
\chi^{\prime}
is supported in
[1/3,2/3]
. We define
\chi_{K}(v):=\chi(K|v|)
.

Proposition 4.4
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
. There exists a kernel
\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})\in\mathscr{C}(\mathbb{R}^{2d}\setminus\{v_{1}=v_{2}\})
such that

(4.18)

\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{2}}{\sqrt{M_{1}}}-{g^{\prime}_{2}}{\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\sqrt{M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}=\int\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})g(v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

with the bound

(4.19)

\left|\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})\right|\leq\frac{C(1+|v_{1}|^{2}+|v_{2}|^{2})\sqrt[13]{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{3}}.

In addition, for almost all
(v_{1},v_{2})
,

(4.20)

\lim_{\hbar\to 0}\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})=\mathscr{K}_{0}(v_{1},v_{2}):=-\left(\nabla_{1}-\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)\otimes\left(\nabla_{2}-\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right):\left(B(v_{1},v_{2})\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\right).

Finally, one can cutoff the singularity at
\{v_{1}=v_{2}\}
: for
\chi_{K}
defined as is Definition
4.2
,

(4.21)

\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g_{2}}{\sqrt{M_{1}}}-{g^{\prime}_{2}}{\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\sqrt{M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\\
=\int\left(\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})-\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})\right)g(v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}+O\left(\left(\tfrac{1}{K}+\hbar K^{3}\right)\|g\|_{\hbar}\|h\|_{\hbar}\right).

where we denote

(4.22)

\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2}):=\frac{K\chi^{\prime}(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}\int_{<v_{1}-v_{2}>^{\perp}}\frac{|k|^{2}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}|^{2}(k)\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})|^{2}}.

Remark 4.2
.

In the sens of distribution, the limit of (
4.22
) is

•

for
d=3
,

\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})\rightharpoonup\int\frac{|2\pi k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\penalty\ \delta_{v_{1}-v_{2}},

•

for
d\geq 4
,

\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})\rightharpoonup 0.

It is not convergent for
d=2
.

Proof.

For
h,g\in L^{2}(\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}}{\left\langle v_{1}\right\rangle})
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{4}{\hbar^{2}}\int

\displaystyle{h_{1}}{\sqrt{M_{2}}}\left({g_{2}}{\sqrt{M_{1}}}-{g^{\prime}_{2}}{\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left(h_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-h_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)\left(g_{2}\sqrt{M_{1}}-g^{\prime}_{2}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=\int h_{1}g_{2}\,\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}},

where, denoting
v_{1}^{\prime\prime}:=v_{1}-\hbar k
and
v_{2}^{\prime\prime}:=v_{2}+\hbar k
, we define

\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2}):=\int\frac{2|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}-\frac{\sqrt{M^{\prime\prime}_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}\\
-\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}+\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}\Bigg)\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k},

We begin by providing a naive bound of
\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}
. If
k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})=0
,

\displaystyle|v_{1}-\hbar k|^{2}+|v_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\tfrac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{|v_{1}+v_{2}|}{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}(v_{1}+v_{2})\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})-\hbar k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})+|\hbar k|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\tfrac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}{2}+\tfrac{|v_{1}+v_{2}|}{4}+\tfrac{1}{2}(v_{1}+v_{2})\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})

\displaystyle\geq\left(\tfrac{1}{2}-\tfrac{1}{2\alpha}\right)|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}+\left(\tfrac{1}{4}-\tfrac{\alpha}{8}\right)|v_{1}+v_{2}|^{2},

for any
\alpha>0
. Optimizing
\alpha=\tfrac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}
, we obtain that
|v_{1}-\hbar k|^{2}+|v_{2}|^{2}\geq\tfrac{|v_{1}|^{2}+|v_{2}|^{2}}{6}
.

Using that
|{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}}(k)|\leq C(1+|k|)^{d+3}
(Assumption
2
), Proposition
3.1
and the previous inequality, one can prove the trivial bound

|\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt[12]{M_{1}M_{2}}}{\hbar^{2}|v_{1}-v_{2}|}.

In order to obtain a better bound, we split
\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})
into two pieces :

(4.23)

\displaystyle\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}

\displaystyle=:\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{<}+\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{>},

(4.24)

\displaystyle\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{>}(v_{1},v_{2})

\displaystyle:=\int_{|k|\geq\frac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}{4\hbar}}\frac{2|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}-\frac{\sqrt{M^{\prime\prime}_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt-\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}+\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}\Bigg)\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

Step
1.
Treatment of
\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{>}
.

Using that
|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}\lesssim\langle k\rangle^{-d-3-\eta}
and Proposition
3.1
,

|\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{>}(v_{1},v_{2})|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt[12]{M_{1}M_{2}}}{\hbar^{2}|v_{1}-v_{2}|}\left\langle\frac{v_{1}-v_{2}}{\hbar}\right\rangle^{-4-\eta}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt[12]{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{3}}\left\langle\frac{v_{1}-v_{2}}{\hbar}\right\rangle^{-1}.

In addition, we have that
\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{>}(v_{1},v_{2})\to 0
.

Step
2.
Bound of
\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{<}
.

For
k\in<v_{1}-v_{2}>^{\perp}
, with
|k|\leq\frac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}{\hbar}
we introduce

\displaystyle{\delta}k:=\left(\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|})-1\right)k+\frac{\hbar|k|^{2}}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|^{2}}(v_{2}-v_{1}),\penalty\ \Lambda(x):=\mathbbm{1}_{|x|\leq 1}\sqrt{1-x^{2}}.

The application

\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\left\{k,\penalty\ k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})=0,|k|\leq\frac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}{\hbar}\right\}\to\left\{\tilde{k},\penalty\ \tilde{k}\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar\tilde{k})=0\right\},\\
k\mapsto\tilde{k}:=k+{\delta}k\end{array}\right.

is a diffeomorphism with Jacobian

\delta_{\tilde{k}\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar\tilde{k})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{\tilde{k}}=\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|})^{d-3}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k,

Note that
|k|=|\tilde{k}|
and
|\delta k|\lesssim\tfrac{\hbar|k|^{2}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}
.

We want to bound

(4.25)

\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}\Lambda\left(\frac{2\hbar|k|}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}\right)^{d-3}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k+\delta k,v_{1})|^{2}}-\frac{\sqrt{M^{\prime\prime}_{1}M_{2}}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}-\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M^{\prime\prime}_{2}}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}\Lambda\left(\frac{2\hbar|k|}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}\right)^{d-3}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k-\delta(-k),v^{\prime\prime}_{1}-\hbar\delta(-k))|^{2}}

Using Proposition
3.1
and Assumption
2
, one can bound the derivative of
\frac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}
: for
r,q\in\mathbb{N}
with
r\leq 2
,

|\nabla_{\hat{k}}^{r}\nabla^{q}_{v_{1}}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}|\lesssim\langle v_{1}\rangle^{q}.

We introduce
k_{s}:=|k|(\cos(s\theta_{k})\hat{k}+\sin(s\theta_{k})\widehat{v_{2}-v_{1}})
with
\theta_{k}:=2\arcsin\tfrac{|\delta k|}{2|k|}
. As
|\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s}\hat{k}_{s}|\lesssim\tfrac{|\delta k|}{|k|}
, we can apply a Taylor formula and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k+\delta k,v_{1})|^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}-\frac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}+\frac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k-\delta(-k),v^{\prime\prime}_{1}-\hbar\delta(-k))|^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s}\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k_{-s},v_{1}+s^{\prime}\hbar k_{-s})|^{2}}+\frac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k_{s-s^{\prime}},v_{1})|^{2}}\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=O\left(\tfrac{|\delta k|^{2}}{|k|^{2}}\langle k\rangle^{2}+|\hbar k|\tfrac{|\delta k|}{|k|}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle v_{1}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\tfrac{\hbar^{2}\langle k\rangle^{4}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{2}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}\right).

Using that
k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})=0
,

\frac{\left(\sqrt{M^{\prime\prime}_{1}}-\sqrt{M_{1}}\right)\sqrt{M_{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}}\left(\sqrt{M^{\prime\prime}_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}}\right)}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}=\frac{\hbar k\cdot\frac{v_{1}-v_{2}}{2}\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}+O\left(\hbar^{2}|k|^{2}\sqrt[12]{M_{1}M_{2}}\right)\\
=O\left(\hbar^{2}|k|^{2}\sqrt[12]{M_{1}M_{2}}\right)

Finally, for
\eta>0
defined in Assumption
2
,

\eqref{eq:quasi derive seconde}\lesssim\hbar^{2}\frac{\sqrt[12]{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|^{2}\langle k\rangle^{d-1+\eta}}.

Integrating with respect to
k
, this gives the bound

(4.26)

\left|\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{<}(v_{1},v_{2})\right|\lesssim\int\frac{\sqrt[12]{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|^{2}\langle k\rangle^{d-1+\eta}}\delta_{(v_{1}-v_{2})\cdot k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k\lesssim\frac{\sqrt[13]{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{3}}.

Step
3.
Convergence of
\mathscr{K}^{<}_{\hbar}
.

It sufficient to prove for almost all
(v_{1},v_{2},k)
the convergence of (
4.25
) when
\hbar\to 0
. Then, the limit of
K^{<}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})
is provided by the dominated convergence theorem.

The simple convergence of (
4.25
) is direct from a Taylor expansion, and the computation are similar than the one for
a priori
estimations (we will not detail them).

In order to identify the limit, we proceed by duality. Let
g,f
two smooth, compactly supported functions.

\displaystyle\int h_{1}g_{2}\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left(h_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-h_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)\left(g_{2}\sqrt{M_{1}}-g^{\prime}_{2}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left(h_{1}^{\prime\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}}-h_{1}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)\left(g_{2}\sqrt{M_{1}^{\prime\prime}}-g^{\prime}_{2}\sqrt{M_{1}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=-2\int k\cdot\left(\nabla h_{1}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}h_{1}\right)\left(\nabla g_{2}+\frac{v_{1}}{2}g_{2}\right)\cdot k\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}+O(\hbar)

\displaystyle=-\int h_{1}g_{2}\left[\left(\nabla_{1}-\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)\otimes\left(\nabla_{2}-\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right):\int\frac{k\otimes k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\right]\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+O(\hbar).

This conclude the proof of (
4.20
).

Step
4.
Cutoff of the singularity at
v_{1}=v_{2}
, estimation of
\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{K}
.

Using the same strategy than for
\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})
,

\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left(h_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-h_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)\left(g_{2}\sqrt{M_{1}}-g^{\prime}_{2}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}\right)\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\\
=\int h_{1}g_{2}\left(\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}+\mathscr{K}^{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

with

\mathscr{K}^{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1},v_{2})=\int\frac{2|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}[\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2}-2\hbar k)-\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})]\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}+\hbar k)}\\
-\left(\frac{\sqrt{M^{\prime\prime}_{1}M_{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})|^{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{M_{1}M^{\prime\prime}_{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\right)[\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2}-\hbar k)-\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})]\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\Bigg)\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k},

We recall that
\nabla^{k}\chi_{K}=O(K^{k})
and

\displaystyle\nabla\chi_{K}(v)=K\chi^{\prime}(K|v|)\frac{v}{|v|}\text{ and }\nabla^{2}\chi_{K}(v)=K^{2}\chi^{\prime\prime}(K|v|)\frac{v^{\otimes 2}}{|v|^{2}}+K\chi^{\prime}(K|v|)\left(\frac{\rm Id}{|v|}-\frac{v^{\otimes 2}}{|v|^{3}}\right).

\bullet
If
k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}+\hbar k)=0
, we have
|v_{2}-v_{1}|=|v_{2}-v_{1}-2\hbar k|
, and then
\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2}-2\hbar k)=\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})
.

\bullet
If
k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})=0
, we have

\displaystyle\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2}-\hbar k)-\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})

\displaystyle=\left(-\hbar k\cdot\nabla\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})+\frac{\hbar^{2}k^{\otimes 2}}{2}:\nabla^{2}\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\right)+O\left((\hbar K|k|)^{3}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{K\chi^{\prime}(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)\hbar^{2}|k|^{2}}{2|v_{1}-v_{2}|}+O\left((\hbar K|k|)^{3}\right)

Integrating with respect to
k
, and using Proposition
3.1
,

\displaystyle\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}^{K}(v_{1},v_{2})=

\displaystyle-2\int\frac{2|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}|k|^{2}\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\frac{K\chi^{\prime}(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)}{2|v_{1}-v_{2}|}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

\displaystyle+\int\left(\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}+\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}+\hbar k)}\right)O\left(\hbar K^{3}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}|k|^{3}\right)\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

(4.27)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\rho_{K}\left(v_{1}-v_{2},\frac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2}\right)+O\left(\tfrac{\hbar K^{3}\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}\right)

We have used that, as
k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})=0
,

|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}=\left|1+{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\int\frac{k\cdot\nabla M(v_{*})}{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{*})-i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{*}\right|^{2}=\left|\varepsilon_{0}\left(M,k,\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2}\right)\right|^{2}.

In order to bound the remainder, we note that for
g,h\in L^{2}(\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v}{\langle v\rangle})
,

\displaystyle\int|g(v_{1})|\tfrac{\hbar K^{3}\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}|h(v_{2})|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\hbar K^{3}\int|\tfrac{g(v_{1})}{\langle v_{1}\rangle^{1/2}}|\tfrac{e^{-\frac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}{8}}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}|\tfrac{h(v_{2})}{\langle v_{2}\rangle^{1/2}}|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\hbar K^{3}\|\tfrac{g(v)}{\langle v\rangle^{1/2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tfrac{h(v)}{\langle v\rangle^{1/2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tfrac{1}{|v|}\exp\left(-\tfrac{|v|^{2}}{8}\right)\|_{L^{1}},

which is bounded as we are in dimension
d\geq 2
.

Step
5.
Estimation near
k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})=0
.

As we live in dimension
d\geq 2
,
\{k|\,k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)=0\}
is of dimension strictly bigger than
1
and

\left|\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left(g_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-g^{\prime}_{1}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)\left(g_{n,2}\sqrt{M_{1}}-g^{\prime}_{2}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)(1-\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2}))\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\right|\\
\begin{split}&\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{|v_{1}-v_{2}|\leq\frac{1}{K}}\left(g_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-g^{\prime}_{1}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)^{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}K}\int\left(g_{1}e^{-\frac{(\hat{k}\cdot v_{1})^{2}}{2}}-g^{\prime}_{1}e^{-\frac{(\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}-\hbar k)^{2}}{2}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{|k|}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}=O\left(\frac{\|g\|_{\hbar}^{2}}{K}\right).\end{split}

This conclude the proof.
∎

Proposition 4.5
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
. There exists a kernel
\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})
such that

(4.28)

\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})g_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}=\int\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})g^{2}(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+O\left(\hbar\|g\|_{\hbar}^{2}\right)

where

(4.29)

\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1}):=\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{1}\cdot\left(v_{1}\int k\otimes k\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}M_{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\langle v_{1}\rangle^{3}}\right).

Proof.

(4.30)

\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})g_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\\
=\frac{1}{2\hbar^{2}}\int\left[(g_{1}^{2}-g_{1}^{\prime 2})\left({M_{2}}-{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)+(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})^{2}\left(\sqrt{{M_{2}}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

Using the change of variable
v_{1}\mapsto v_{1}-\hbar k
,

\eqref{eq:2.33}=\int g_{1}^{2}\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{\hbar}(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})^{2}\left(\sqrt{{M_{2}}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V},

where we define

\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{\hbar}(v_{1}):=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\pi}\hbar^{2}}\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{|k|}\left(\frac{e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}-e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}-\frac{e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}-e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+2\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1}-\hbar k)|^{2}}\right)\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}.

Step
1.
We denote

(4.31)

\varpi(k,v_{1}\cdot\hat{k},\hbar):=\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}|k|^{2}}\left(\frac{e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}-e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}-\frac{e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}-e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+2\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1}-\hbar k)|^{2}}\right).

Using a Taylor formula, and introducing
w:=v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}
,

\displaystyle\varpi(k,w,\hbar)=

\displaystyle\int_{[0,1]^{2}}\frac{(w+(s+s^{\prime})\hbar|k|)^{2}-1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,w\hat{k})|^{2}}e^{-\frac{((w+(s+s^{\prime})\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\int_{[0,1]^{2}}\frac{(w+s\hbar|k|)}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,(w+s^{\prime}\hbar|k|)\hat{k})|^{4}}\hat{k}\cdot\nabla_{v}|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,(w+s^{\prime}\hbar|k|)\hat{k})|^{2}e^{-\frac{(w+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s^{\prime}}.

One have
\forall(k,w,\hbar)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}\times(0,\infty)
,

(4.32)

\displaystyle|\varpi(k,w,\hbar)|\lesssim\int_{0}^{2}e^{-\frac{(w+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}{4}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s},

(4.33)

\displaystyle\varpi(k,w,0)=\left(\frac{w^{2}-1}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,w\hat{k})|^{2}}+\frac{w\hat{k}\cdot\nabla_{v}|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,w\hat{k})|^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,w\hat{k})|^{4}}\right)e^{-\frac{w^{2}}{2}}=\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}w}\left(\frac{-w}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,w\hat{k})|^{2}}e^{-\frac{w^{2}}{2}}\right),

(4.34)

\displaystyle|\varpi(k,w,\hbar)-\varpi(k,w,0)|\lesssim\hbar(1+|k|)\int_{0}^{2}e^{-\frac{(w+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}{4}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\penalty\ {\rm for}\penalty\ \hbar|k|\leq 1.

This last estimate can be deduce from
3.1
and using the same method than before.

Using the change of variable
k\mapsto(\sigma,x,|k|)
introduce in (
4.7
), we have

(4.35)

\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{\hbar}(v_{1})=\frac{|\mathbb{S}^{d-2}|}{2(2\pi)^{d+\frac{1}{2}}}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{d-3}{2}}\varpi(k,|v_{1}|x,\hbar)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}

We introduce

(4.36)

\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1}):=\frac{|\mathbb{S}^{d-2}|}{2(2\pi)^{d+\frac{1}{2}}}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{d-3}{2}}\varpi(k,|v_{1}|x,0)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}.

We split the difference
\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{\hbar}(v_{1})-\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})
into two part

(4.37)

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathbb{S}^{d-2}|}{2(2\pi)^{d+\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{|k|>|\hbar|^{-1}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{d-3}{2}}\left(\varpi(k,|v_{1}|x,\hbar)-\varpi(k,|v_{1}|x,0)\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}

(4.38)

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathbb{S}^{d-2}|}{2(2\pi)^{d+\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{|k|<|\hbar|^{-1}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{d-3}{2}}\left(\varpi(k,|v_{1}|x,\hbar)-\varpi(k,|v_{1}|x,0)\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}

We get the following bound: using (
4.32
),

\begin{split}\eqref{eq:tilde(K)1}&\lesssim\int_{0}^{2}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|k|>|\hbar|^{-1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}e^{-\frac{(x|v_{1}|+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}{4}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\\
&\lesssim\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|k|>|\hbar|^{-1}\end{subarray}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-\frac{(x|v_{1}|)^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\lesssim\frac{\hbar}{<v_{1}>},\end{split}

and using (
4.34
)

\eqref{eq:tilde(K)2}\lesssim\hbar\int_{0}^{2}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}(1+|k|)e^{-\frac{(x|v_{1}|+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}{4}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\lesssim\frac{\hbar}{<v_{1}>}.

The second line is obtained using (
4.34
). This gives the approximation results

(4.39)

\mathscr{K}_{\hbar}(v_{1})=\mathscr{K}_{0}(v_{1})+O(\tfrac{\hbar}{\langle v_{1}\rangle}).

In order to bound
\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})
, we split it into two parts:

(4.40)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})=

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathbb{S}^{d-2}|}{2(2\pi)^{d+\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{|k|<|\hbar|^{-1}}\left(\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{d-3}{2}}-1\right){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}\varpi(k,|v_{1}|x,0)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}

(4.41)

\displaystyle+\frac{|\mathbb{S}^{d-2}|}{2(2\pi)^{d+\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{|k|<|\hbar|^{-1}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)|k|^{d}\varpi(k,|v_{1}|x,0)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}.

Using that for
|x|\leq\tfrac{1}{2}
,
(1-(1-x^{2})^{(d-3)/2})\lesssim x^{2}
, and Inequality (
4.32
),

\eqref{eq:tilde(K)3}\lesssim\int_{|x|\leq\frac{1}{2}}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}|k|^{d}x^{2}e^{-\frac{(x|v_{1}|)^{2}}{4}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}+\int_{|x|\leq\frac{1}{2}}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}|k|^{d}\left|\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{\frac{d-3}{2}}-1\right|e^{-\frac{x^{2}|v_{1}|^{2}}{8}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{|k|}\\
\lesssim\int x^{2}e^{-\frac{x^{2}|v_{1}|^{2}}{8}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{x}\lesssim\frac{1}{<v_{1}>^{3}}.

Using the equality (
4.33
),

\eqref{eq:tilde(K)4}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{|k|<1/\hbar}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}|k|^{d}\int_{-1}^{1}\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}w}\left(\frac{-w}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,w\hat{k})|^{2}}e^{-\frac{w^{2}}{2}}\right)\Bigg|_{w=|v_{1}|x}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}x\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}|k|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\sigma=O\left(\frac{e^{-\frac{|v_{1}|^{2}}{4}}}{\langle v_{1}\rangle}\right).

We deduce that
\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})=O(\tfrac{1}{\langle v_{1}\rangle^{3}})
.

Step
2.
We treat now the remaining part

\displaystyle\int(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})^{2}\left(\sqrt{{M_{2}}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\!\!\int(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})^{2}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}{8}}|k|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\int_{|k|\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\hbar}}}\int g_{1}^{2}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}{8}}|k|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}+\int_{0}^{1}\int_{|k|\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\hbar}}}\int(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})^{2}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+s\hbar|k|)^{2}}{8}}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{\hbar|k|}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\hbar\|g\|^{2}_{\hbar}.

Step
3.
We need to identify the limit. As in Step 3 of the proof of Proposition
4.4
, we use a duality method. For
g
a smooth test function with compact support,

\displaystyle\int g_{1}^{2}\tilde{K}(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\hbar^{2}}\int(g_{1}^{2}-g_{1}^{\prime 2})\left({M_{2}}-{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\hbar^{2}}\int(g^{\prime\prime 2}_{1}-g_{1}^{2})\left({M_{2}}-{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int k\cdot\nabla g^{2}_{1}k\cdot v_{2}M_{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}+O(\hbar)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int g_{1}^{2}{\rm div}_{1}\left(\left(\int k\otimes k\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}M_{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\right)\cdot v_{1}\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+O(\hbar)

This conclude the proof.
∎

4.3.
Dissipation estimates

We denote
w_{0}=\sqrt{M}
,
(w_{i})_{i\in[1,d]}=({v\cdot{\mathbf{e}}_{i}}\sqrt{M})
(where
({\mathbf{e}}_{i})_{i}
is an orthogonal bases of
\mathbb{R}^{d}
) and
w_{d+1}:=\frac{|v|^{2}-d}{\sqrt{2d}}\sqrt{M}
. We introduce
\pi_{0}
the
L^{2}
-orthonormal on
<w_{0},\cdots,w_{d+1}>
,

\pi_{0}[g]:=\sum_{i=0}^{d+1}\left(\int w_{i}g\right)w_{i}.

Proposition 4.6
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
.

There exists a constant
C>0
independant of
\hbar
and an
\hbar_{0}>0
such that for any
\hbar<\hbar_{0}
,
g\in\mathscr{H}_{\hbar}
, we have the lower bound

(4.42)

C\int g\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}g\geq\|(1-\pi_{0})g\|_{\hbar}^{2}.

Proof.

We proceed by contradiction. Suppose that there exists a decreasing sequence
\hbar_{n}\to 0
and a sequence
(g_{n})_{n}
such that
\pi_{0}g_{n}=0
,
\|g_{n}\|_{\hbar_{n}}=1
and
\int g_{n}\mathscr{L}_{\hbar_{n}}g_{n}\to 0
.

Using Proposition
4.3
, we deduce that up to an extraction, the sequence
(g_{n})
converge weakly to
g_{\infty}
in all the
\mathscr{H}_{\hbar}
, and there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\forall\hbar\in(0,\hbar_{0}),\penalty\ \|g_{\infty}\|^{2}_{\hbar}<C.

Lemma 4.7
.

The sequence
g_{n}
converges strongly in
L^{2}_{\rm loc}
and

(4.43)

\|g_{\infty}\|_{0}^{2}\leq 1.

Proof.

Fix
K>0
. We denote
B(K)
the ball of radius
K
and of center
0
.
For any
\hbar\in(0,1)
,
k\in\overline{B(1)}\setminus B(\tfrac{2}{3})
, and any test function
g
, one have

\displaystyle\int_{B(K)}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\in\frac{k}{2}+B(\frac{1}{4})\\
v_{1}\in B(K)\end{subarray}}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar{k^{\prime}})-g(v_{1}))^{2}+(g(v_{1}+\hbar{k^{\prime}})-g(v_{1}-\hbar k))^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}k^{\prime}\in B(\frac{3}{4})\setminus B(\frac{1}{12})\\
v_{1}\in B(K)\end{subarray}}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar{k^{\prime}})-g(v_{1}))^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle e^{K^{2}}\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left({g(v_{1})}-{g(v_{1}+\hbar k)}\right)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{{k}})^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k^{\prime}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\leq C_{K}

for some constant
C_{K}
depending on
K
. For any
k\in B(\tfrac{1}{3})
,
k=\hat{k}-(1-|k|)\hat{k}
. As
(1-|k|)\in[\tfrac{2}{3},1]
,

\displaystyle\int_{B(K)}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar k)-g(v_{1}))^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{B(K)}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar\hat{k})-g(v_{1}))^{2}+(g(v_{1}+\hbar\hat{k})-g(v_{1}-\hbar(1-|k|)\hat{k}))^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{B(2K)}\frac{(g(v_{1}+\hbar\hat{k})-g(v_{1}))^{2}+(g(v_{1}+\hbar(1-|k|)\hat{k})-g(v_{1}))^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k^{\prime}}\lesssim 2C_{K}

We deduce the uniform continuity of the sequence
(g_{n})_{n}
: for any
\hbar>0
, take some
\hbar^{\prime}\in(0,\hbar)
.

\lim_{\begin{subarray}{c}\hbar^{\prime}\to 0\\
\hbar^{\prime}\leq\hbar\end{subarray}}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}|k|\leq\frac{\hbar^{\prime}}{3}\\
n\geq 0\end{subarray}}\|g_{n}(k+\cdot)-g_{n}(\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(B(K))}\leq C_{K}\hbar+\lim_{\hbar^{\prime}\to 0}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}|k|\leq\frac{\hbar^{\prime}}{3}\\
n,\,\hbar_{n}\geq\hbar\end{subarray}}\|g_{n}(k+\cdot)-g_{n}(\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(B(K))}\leq C_{K}\hbar.

One can apply Rellich-Kondrakov theorem. Hence the sequence
(g_{n})_{n}
converges strongly in
L^{2}(B(K))
.

In addition, as
\|g_{\infty}\|_{\hbar}
is uniformly bounded, we deduce that for any
k\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\|g_{\infty}(k+\cdot)-g_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(B(K))}\leq|k|C_{K},

which is a criteria that proves
g_{\infty}\in H^{1}_{\rm loc}
, and
\frac{\left({g_{\infty}(\cdot+\hbar k)}-{g_{\infty}(\cdot)}\right)}{\hbar}
converges weakly to
k\cdot\nabla g_{\infty}
in
L^{2}(B(K))
.

For any
K>0
,

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{|v_{1}|\leq K}\left[\left({g_{n,1}}-{g^{\prime}_{n,1}}\right)^{2}M_{2}+g_{n,1}^{2}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{|v_{1}|\leq K}\left[\left(\frac{{g_{n}(v_{1}+\hbar k)}-{g_{n}(v_{1})}}{\hbar}\right)^{2}+\frac{g_{n,1}^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\left(1-e^{-\frac{2\hbar v_{1}\cdot k+\hbar^{2}|k|^{2}}{2}}\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{|v_{1}|\leq K}\left[\left(\frac{{g_{n}(v_{1}+\hbar k)}-{g_{n}(v_{1})}}{\hbar}\right)^{2}+g_{n,1}^{2}\left(v_{1}\cdot k\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}{2}}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+O\left(\hbar\|g_{n}\|^{2}_{\hbar}\right)

One can take the infimum limit as
n\to 0
(as everything is convex, strongly continuous):

\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{|v_{1}|\leq K}\left[\left(k\cdot\nabla g_{\infty}(v_{1})\right)^{2}+g_{\infty}(v_{1})^{2}\left(v_{1}\cdot k\right)^{2}\right]e^{-\frac{(v_{1}\cdot\hat{k})^{2}}{2}}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\leq 1

Finally we take the limit
K\to\infty
by monotone convergence. This conclude the proof.
∎

In the following, we prove the convergence of
\int g_{n}\mathscr{L}_{\hbar_{n}}g_{n}
to
0
. In dimension
3
, we will need a cut-off function
\chi_{K}
introduce in Definition
4.2
. If the dimension is bigger than
4
, this cut-off function is not needed, and we consider
\chi_{K}\equiv 1
.

We recall the decomposition of
\int g_{n}\mathscr{L}_{\hbar_{n}}g_{n}
: for any
K>0
,

\displaystyle\int g_{n}\mathscr{L}_{\hbar_{n}}g_{n}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\hbar_{n}}^{2}}\int\left[\left(g_{n,1}-g^{\prime}_{n,1}\right)^{2}M_{2}+g_{n,1}^{\prime 2}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar_{n}k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar_{n}}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\hbar_{n}^{2}}\int\left[\left(g_{n,1}-g^{\prime}_{n,1}\right)g_{n,1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar_{n}k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar_{n}}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\hbar_{n}^{2}}\int\left(g_{n,1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-g^{\prime}_{n,1}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)\left(g_{n,2}\sqrt{M_{1}}-g^{\prime}_{n,2}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

•

Using, the first line is equal to

\frac{1}{{\hbar_{n}}^{2}}\int\left[\left(g_{n,1}-g^{\prime}_{n,1}\right)^{2}M_{2}+g_{n,1}^{\prime 2}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar_{n}k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar_{n}}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V=\frac{\|g_{n}\|_{\hbar_{n}}}{2}=\frac{1}{2}.

•

For second line, using Proposition
4.5
and that

\int_{|v_{1}|>K}g_{n}^{2}(v_{1})\left|\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})\right|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\lesssim\int_{|v_{1}|>K}\frac{g_{n}(v_{1})^{2}}{\langle v_{1}\rangle^{3}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}=O(\tfrac{1}{K}),

one have

\frac{2}{\hbar_{n}^{2}}\int\left[\left(g_{n,1}-g^{\prime}_{n,1}\right)g_{n,1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(\sqrt{M_{2}}-\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar_{n}k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar_{n}}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\\
=\int_{|v_{1}|\leq K}g_{n}^{2}(v_{1})\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+O\left(\tfrac{1}{K}+\hbar_{n}\right).

•

For the third line, using Proposition
4.4
, we perform the decomposition

\frac{2}{\hbar_{n}^{2}}\int\left(g_{n,1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-g^{\prime}_{n,1}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\right)\left(g_{n,2}\sqrt{M_{1}}-g^{\prime}_{n,2}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar_{n}k)}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar_{n}}(M,v_{1},k)|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\\
=\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|v_{1}|<K\\
|v_{2}|<K\end{subarray}}\left(\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\mathscr{K}_{\hbar_{n}}(v_{1},v_{2})-\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})\right)g_{n}(v_{1})g_{n}(v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}+O\left(\tfrac{1}{K}+K^{3}\hbar_{n}\right).

We used that

\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\{|v_{1}|\geq K\}\end{subarray}}\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\left(\left|\mathscr{K}_{\hbar_{n}}(v_{1},v_{2})\right|+\left|\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})\right|\right)\left|g_{n,1}\right|\left|g_{n,2}\right|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\\
\lesssim\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|v_{1}|\geq K\\
|v_{1}-v_{2}|>\frac{1}{3K}\end{subarray}}\frac{\sqrt[5]{M_{1}M_{2}}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{3}}\left|g_{n,1}\right|\left|g_{n,2}\right|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}=O(\tfrac{1}{K}).

We can take now the limit
n\to\infty
, using that
g_{n}
converges strongly to
g_{\infty}
in
L^{2}_{\rm loc}
:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|v_{1}|<K\\
|v_{2}|<K\end{subarray}}\left(\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\mathscr{K}_{0}(v_{1},v_{2})-\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})\right)g_{\infty}(v_{1})g_{\infty}(v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt+\int_{|v_{1}|\leq K}g_{\infty}(v_{1})^{2}\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+O(\tfrac{1}{K})

(4.44)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\|g_{\infty}\|_{0}^{2}}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\int\left(\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\mathscr{K}_{0}(v_{1},v_{2})-\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})\right)g_{\infty}(v_{1})g_{\infty}(v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 241.84842pt+\int g_{\infty}(v_{1})^{2}\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+O(\tfrac{1}{K}).

One can perform an integration by part and

\int g_{\infty}(v_{1})^{2}\tilde{\mathscr{K}}_{0}(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}=\frac{1}{2}\int g_{\infty}(v_{1})^{2}\nabla_{1}\cdot\left(v_{1}\int k\otimes k\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}M_{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\right)\\
=-\int\nabla g_{\infty}(v_{1})B(v_{1},v_{2})(g_{\infty}(v_{1})v_{1})M_{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}.}

In the same way, using that
B(v_{1},v_{2})(v_{1}-v_{2})=0
,

\displaystyle\left(\nabla_{1}-\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)\otimes\left(\nabla_{2}-\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right):\left(\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}B(v_{1},v_{2})\right)+\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\mathscr{K}_{0}(v_{1}-v_{2})

\displaystyle=-\nabla^{2}\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2}):B(v_{1},v_{2})\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}+\nabla\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\otimes\left(\nabla_{2}-\nabla_{1}-\tfrac{v_{2}-v_{1}}{2}\right):\left(\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}B(v_{1},v_{2})\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\left(-\nabla^{2}\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2}):B(v_{1},v_{2})-\nabla\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\cdot\left(\left(\nabla_{1}-\nabla_{2}\right)\cdot B(v_{1},v_{2})\right)\right)

Using that
\chi_{K}(v)=\chi(K|v|)
,

\nabla^{2}\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2}):B(v_{1},v_{2})=\frac{K\chi^{\prime}(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}\int_{<v_{1}-v_{2}>^{\perp}}\frac{|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}|k|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,\frac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2},k)|^{2}}=\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2}),

where
\rho_{K}
has been introduce in (
4.22
).

\displaystyle{\rm div}_{v_{1}}\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\otimes k}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int k\cdot\nabla_{v_{1}}\left(\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\right)k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}+\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}|k|^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}k\delta^{\prime}_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int k\cdot\nabla_{v_{1}}\left(\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\right)k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}-\int\frac{v_{1}-v_{2}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}\cdot\nabla_{k}\left(\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}|k|^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\right)k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt-\frac{v_{1}-v_{2}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}|k|^{2}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(M,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}.

Using that
\nabla\chi_{K}(v)=K\chi^{\prime}(K|v|)\tfrac{v}{|v|}
, one have

-\nabla\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\cdot\left(\left(\nabla_{1}-\nabla_{2}\right)\cdot B(v_{1},v_{2})\right)=2\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2}).

Finally,

\displaystyle\int

\displaystyle\left(\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\mathscr{K}_{0}(v_{1},v_{2})-\rho_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2},\tfrac{v_{1}+v_{2}}{2})\right)g_{\infty}(v_{1})g_{\infty}(v_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int g_{\infty}(v_{1})g_{\infty}(v_{2})\left(\nabla_{1}-\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)\otimes\left(\nabla_{2}-\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right):\left[\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})B(v_{1},v_{2})\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\right]\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int\left(\nabla_{1}+\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)g_{\infty}(v_{1})B(v_{1},v_{2})\left(\nabla_{2}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right)g_{\infty}(v_{2})\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}
