\displaystyle+\int\left(1-\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\right)\left(\nabla_{1}+\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)g_{\infty}(v_{1})B(v_{1},v_{2})\left(\nabla_{2}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right)g_{\infty}(v_{2})\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}.

One can bound the est using the Young inequality:

\int\left|1-\chi_{K}(v_{1}-v_{2})\right|\left|\left(\nabla_{1}+\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)g_{\infty}(v_{1})\right|\left|B(v_{1},v_{2})\right|\left|\left(\nabla_{2}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right)g_{\infty}(v_{2})\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\right|\\
\lesssim\left\|\frac{1-\chi_{K}(v)}{|v|}\right\|_{L^{1}}\left\|\left(\nabla_{2}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right)g_{\infty}(v_{2})\sqrt{M_{2}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}}=O(\tfrac{1}{K}).

Hence, taking the limit
K\to\infty
in (
4.44
),

0\geq\frac{1}{2}\int\left[\left(\nabla_{1}+\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)g_{\infty,1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-\left(\nabla_{2}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right)g_{\infty,2}\sqrt{M_{1}}\right]B(v_{1},v_{2})\\
\left[\left(\nabla_{1}+\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)g_{\infty,1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-\left(\nabla_{2}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right)g_{\infty,2}\sqrt{M_{1}}\right]\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

As this term is non-negative, it is zero. We deduce that (see
[
DW23
]
, Subset 2.2 of the proof of Proposition 2.5),
g_{\infty}\in<w_{0},\cdots,w_{d+1}>
, which contradict the fact that
\pi_{0}(g_{\infty})=0
. This conclude the proof.
∎

Proposition 4.8
.

For any test function
g

(4.45)

\displaystyle\left\|\pi_{0}\left[\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)g\right]\right\|_{\hbar}\lesssim\min(\|g\|_{\hbar},\|g\|)

Proof.

We recall that

\pi_{0}[g]:=\sum_{i=0}^{d+1}\left(\int w_{i}g\right)w_{i}

As
|w_{i}|=O(\sqrt[4]{M})
and
\|w_{i}\|_{\hbar}\lesssim\|w_{i}\|_{0}<\infty
, we can conclude.
∎

4.4.
Non-linear estimation and Cauchy theory for pertubative solution of the quantum Lenard-Balescu equation

In the following, we denote
\mathscr{H}^{r}_{\hbar}
the Hilbert space of norm

\|g\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}:=\sum_{r^{\prime}=0}^{r}\left\|\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}g\right\|^{2}_{\hbar}.

The present section is dedicated to bound
\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}
and
\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}
.

We denote

(4.46)

\mathbf{H}_{k}(p)[v_{1}]:=\int\frac{p(v_{3})-p(v_{3}-k)}{k\cdot(v_{3}-v_{1}-k)+i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{3}}.

Proposition 4.9
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
. For any test functions
f
,
g
,
h
, and
p
, the three following bounds hold:

(4.47)

\displaystyle\int\left|\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left({f}\sqrt{M_{*}}\right)\right|\left|\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(gh_{*}\right)\right|\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\lesssim\|f\|_{\hbar}\min(\|g\|_{\hbar}\|h\|_{3},\|g\|_{3}\|h\|_{\hbar}),

(4.48)

\displaystyle\int\left|\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left({f}{\sqrt{M_{*}}}\right)\right|\left|\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left({gh_{*}}\right)\right|\left|\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)\right|\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\lesssim\|f\|_{\hbar}\|p\|_{\hbar}\|g\|_{2}\|h\|_{2},

(4.49)

\left\|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{k,v_{1}}}\lesssim\|p\|_{2}.

Proof.

Step
1.
We begin by (
4.47
). Using the symmetry of the expression, it is sufficient to bound

(4.50)

\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left|f_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-f_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right|\left|g_{1}h_{2}-g_{1}^{\prime}h_{2}^{\prime}\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

We can split it into four parts:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left|(f_{1}-f_{1}^{\prime})\sqrt[4]{M_{2}}\right|\left|\sqrt[4]{M_{2}}g_{1}(h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime})\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left|f_{1}^{\prime}(\sqrt[4]{M_{2}^{\prime}}-\sqrt[4]{M_{2}})\right|\left|(\sqrt[4]{M_{2}^{\prime}}+\sqrt[4]{M_{2}})g_{1}(h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime})\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left|(f_{1}-f_{1}^{\prime})\sqrt[4]{M_{2}}\right|\left|\sqrt[4]{M_{2}}(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})h_{2}^{\prime}\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left|f_{1}^{\prime}(\sqrt[4]{M_{2}}-\sqrt[4]{M_{2}^{\prime}})\right|\left|(\sqrt[4]{M_{2}}+\sqrt[4]{M^{\prime}_{2}})(g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime})h_{2}^{\prime}\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

Applying the Cauchy–Schwartz inequality to each term of the sum, we obtain

\eqref{eq:premier terme quadratique}\lesssim A_{1}(f)(A_{2}(g,h)+A_{2}(h,g))

where we denote

\displaystyle A_{1}(f):=\int e^{\frac{|v_{2}\cdot k|^{2}}{4|k|^{2}}}\left[(f_{1}-f_{1}^{\prime})^{2}M_{2}+(f_{1}^{\prime})^{2}(\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}-\sqrt{M_{2}})^{2}\right]\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}}{\hbar^{2}}.

\displaystyle A_{2}(g,h):=\int e^{-\frac{|v_{2}\cdot k|^{2}}{4|k|^{2}}}g^{2}_{1}(h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime})^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V}{\hbar^{2}}.

For a fix
k\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we introduce the notation
L^{p}_{\shortparallel}(L^{q}_{\perp}):=L^{p}(<\hat{k}>,L^{q}(<\hat{k}>^{\perp}))
. Using Proposition
4.2
and usual Sobolev injection theorem, these terms can be bound by

\displaystyle A_{1}(f)\lesssim\|f\|_{\hbar}^{2},

\displaystyle A_{2}(g,h)\lesssim\|\nabla h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{2}_{\perp})}\int g^{2}_{1}e^{-\frac{|v_{2}\cdot k|^{2}}{4|k|^{2}}}|k|^{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\leq\|h\|_{2}^{2}\|g\|_{\hbar}^{2}.

This conclude the proof of (
4.47
).

Step
2.
We treat now (
4.48
). We introduce the orthogonal decomposition
v=v^{\shortparallel}+v^{\perp}
, where
v^{\shortparallel}
lives in
\mathbb{R}k
. Remark that
\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)(v_{1})=\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)(v_{1}^{\shortparallel})
.

We bound first
\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}
: using the
L^{2}\to L^{2}
bound on the Hilbert transform,

\begin{split}\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}|\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)(v_{1}^{\shortparallel})|^{2}&\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v^{\shortparallel}_{1}}\lesssim\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}\left|\int_{<k^{\perp}>}(p_{1}\sqrt{M_{1}}-p_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{1}})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}^{\perp}\right|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v^{\shortparallel}_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\\
&\lesssim\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}\left|\int_{<k^{\perp}>}(p_{1}e^{-\frac{|v_{1}^{|\hskip-1.0pt|}|^{2}}{4}}-p_{1}^{\prime}e^{-\frac{|v_{1}^{|\hskip-1.0pt|}+\hbar k|^{2}}{4}})e^{-\frac{|v_{1}^{\perp}|^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}^{\perp}\right|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v^{\shortparallel}_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k\\
&\lesssim\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}^{2}(k)}{|k|}(p_{1}e^{-\frac{|v_{1}^{|\hskip-1.0pt|}|^{2}}{4}}-p_{1}^{\prime}e^{-\frac{|v_{1}^{|\hskip-1.0pt|}+\hbar k|^{2}}{4}})e^{-\frac{|v_{1}^{\perp}|^{2}}{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\\
&\lesssim\hbar^{2}\|p\|_{\hbar}^{2}\end{split}

Hence,

\displaystyle\int\left[\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)(h_{1}g_{2}-h_{1}^{\prime}g_{2}^{\prime})\right]^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\left[\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}\sqrt{M}p)(v_{1}^{\shortparallel},k)(h(v_{1}^{\shortparallel}+v_{1}^{\perp})g(v_{1}^{\shortparallel}+\hbar k+v_{2}^{\perp}))-(h(v_{1}^{\shortparallel}+\hbar k+v_{1}^{\perp})g(v_{1}^{\shortparallel}+v_{1}^{\perp}))\right]^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{|k|\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}^{\shortparallel}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v^{\perp}_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v^{\perp}_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|p\|_{\hbar}^{2}\|g\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{2}_{\perp})}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{2}_{\perp})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|p\|_{\hbar}\|g\|_{1}\|h\|_{1}\right)^{2}.

Applying the Cauchy–Schwartz inequality,

\eqref{eq:ix}\lesssim A_{1}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\left[\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)(h_{1}g_{2}-h_{1}^{\prime}g_{2}^{\prime})\right]^{2}\tfrac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|f\|_{\hbar}\|p\|_{\hbar}\|g\|_{1}\|h\|_{1}.

Step
3.
For any test function
p
, using the orthogonal decomposition
v_{1}=v^{\shortparallel}_{1}+v_{1}^{\perp}
,
v_{1}^{\shortparallel}\in\mathbb{R}\hat{k}
, using trace inequality,

\displaystyle\left\|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{k,v_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\nabla_{v_{1}}\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(\sqrt{M}p)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{k}(L^{2}(\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}^{|\hskip-1.0pt|})}=\left\|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}\left(\sqrt{M}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)p\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{k}(L^{2}(\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}^{|\hskip-1.0pt|})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)}{\hbar|k|}\int\left(\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)p(v_{*}+\hbar k)-\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)p(v_{*})\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}^{\perp}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k},L^{2}(\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}}))}\lesssim\left\|p\right\|_{2}.

∎

Proposition 4.10
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
. Denoting

, for

three tests functions

(4.51)

\left|\left\langle\left(-\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\right)^{r}\left(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\right)^{r}f,\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g,h)\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\left(1+\|h\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\right)^{r+1}\left(\|g\|_{\hbar,r}\|h\|_{\tilde{r}-1}+\|g\|_{\hbar,r}\|h\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\right)\|\left\|f\right\|_{\hbar,r},

(4.52)

\left\langle\left(-\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\right)^{r}\left(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\right)^{r}f,\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}g\right\rangle=\left\langle\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}f,\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}\left(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\right)^{r}g\right\rangle+O\left(\|g\|_{\hbar,{r}-1}\|\left\|f\right\|_{\hbar,r}\right),

(4.53)

\left|\left\langle\left(-\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}f\left[\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g,h)-\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(\tilde{g},\tilde{h})\right]\right\rangle\right|\\
\lesssim\left\|f\right\|_{\hbar,r}\left(1+\|h\|_{\tilde{r}-1}+\|\tilde{h}\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\right)^{r+1}\Big(\|g-\tilde{g}\|_{\hbar,r}+\left(\|g\|_{\tilde{r}-1}+\|\tilde{g}\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\right)\|h-\tilde{h}\|_{\hbar,r}\\
+\left(\|h\|_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{h}\|_{\hbar,r}\right)\|g-\tilde{g}\|_{\tilde{r}-1}+\left(\|g\|_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{g}\|_{\hbar,r}\right)\|h-\tilde{h}\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\Big).

Proof.

As
(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2})g(v_{1}^{\prime})=\nabla g(v_{1}^{\prime})
, we has

\displaystyle\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\frac{\left(\nabla+\frac{v}{2}\right)g}{\sqrt{M}}\right)=\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\nabla\frac{g}{\sqrt{M}}\right)=(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2})\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\frac{g}{\sqrt{M}}\right),

\displaystyle(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2})\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left({gh_{*}}{\sqrt{MM_{*}}}\right)=\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\left[\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)gh_{*}+g\left(\nabla_{*}-\frac{v_{*}}{2}\right)h_{*}\right]\sqrt{MM_{*}}\right).

In addition,

(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}=\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\left(-k+k\right)=0.

Hence, for
f,g,h,F=M+\sqrt{M}p
measurable functions

\displaystyle(-1)^{r}\int\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\frac{\left(\nabla+\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}f}{\sqrt{M}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{d-2}}{|\varepsilon(F,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left({gh_{*}}\right)\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{r}\int(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2})^{r}\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\frac{\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}f}{\sqrt{M}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{d-2}}{|\varepsilon(F,k,v_{1})|^{2}}\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left({gh_{*}}\right)\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1}+r_{2}+r_{3}=r}\binom{r}{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}}\int\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\frac{\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}f}{\sqrt{M}}\right){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{d-2}\frac{\partial^{r_{1}}|\varepsilon(F,k,v_{1})|^{-2}}{(\partial v_{1})^{r_{1}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r_{2}}g\left(\nabla_{*}-\frac{v_{*}}{2}\right)^{r_{3}}h_{*}\right)\sqrt{M_{1}M_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

Using the Faà di Bruno formula, and
F=M+\sqrt{M}p

\frac{\partial^{r_{1}}|\varepsilon(F,k,v_{1})|^{-2}}{(\partial v_{1})^{r_{1}}}=\sum_{k,l=0}^{r_{1}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma_{1}+\cdots+\gamma_{k}\\
+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}+\cdots+\tilde{\gamma}_{l}=r_{1}\\
\gamma_{i},\tilde{\gamma_{i}}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{(-1)^{k+l}r_{1}!k!l!}{(\gamma_{1}+\cdots\gamma_{k})!(\tilde{\gamma}_{1}+\cdots+\tilde{\gamma}_{l})!}\frac{\prod_{j=1}^{k}\frac{\partial^{\gamma_{j}}\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F,k,v_{1})}{(\partial v_{1})^{{\gamma}_{j}}}\prod_{j=1}^{l}\frac{\partial^{\tilde{\gamma}_{j}}\overline{\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F,k,v_{1})}}{(\partial v_{1})^{\tilde{\gamma}_{j}}}}{\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F,k,v_{1})^{k+1}\overline{\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F,k,v_{1})}^{l+1}}

We recall that (for
\mathbf{H}_{k}
defining in (
4.46
))

\frac{\partial^{\gamma_{j}}\varepsilon(F,k,v)}{(\partial v_{1})^{\gamma_{j}}}-\frac{\partial^{\gamma_{j}}\varepsilon(\tilde{F},k,v)}{(\partial v_{1})^{\gamma_{j}}}={{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}}(k)}\int\frac{\nabla^{\gamma_{j}}\left[\sqrt{M}(p-\tilde{p})\right](v_{*})-\nabla^{\gamma_{j}}\left[\sqrt{M}(p-\tilde{p})\right](v_{*}-\hbar k)}{\hbar k\cdot(v-v_{*}-\hbar k)+i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{*}}\\
={{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}\left(\sqrt{M}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{\gamma_{j}}(p-\tilde{p})\right)}

We deduce that

\left|\frac{\partial^{r}|\varepsilon(F,k,v_{1})|^{-2}}{(\partial v_{1})^{\gamma_{j}}}-\frac{\partial^{r}|\varepsilon(\tilde{F},k,v_{1})|^{-2}}{(\partial v_{1})^{r}}\right|\\
\lesssim_{r}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|\left(1+\left\|p\right\|_{\max(4,r-1)}+\left\|\tilde{p}\right\|_{\max(4,r-1)}\right)^{r}\left(\sum_{s=0}^{2}\left|\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}\left(\sqrt{M}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r-r}(p-\tilde{p})\right)\right|+\left\|\tilde{p}-p\right\|_{r-1}\right).

Using that
r_{1}+r_{2}+r_{3}=r
, and Proposition
4.9
denoting
\tilde{r}:=\max\{5,r\}

\int\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\frac{\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}\left(-\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r}f}{\sqrt{M}}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}\\
\times\left[|\varepsilon(F,k,v_{1})|^{-2}-|\varepsilon(\tilde{F},k,v_{1})|^{-2}\right]\Delta\hskip-6.0pt\Delta\left(\frac{gh_{*}}{\sqrt{MM_{*}}}\right)M_{1}M_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
\lesssim\left(1+\left\|p\right\|_{\tilde{r}-1}+\left\|\tilde{p}\right\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\right)^{r+1}\left\|f\right\|_{\hbar,r}\left(\left\|g\right\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\left\|h\right\|_{r,\hbar}+\left\|h\right\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\left\|g\right\|_{r,\hbar}+\left\|g\right\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\left\|h\right\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\|p-\tilde{p}\|_{r,\hbar}\right)

One can conclude by choosing suitable
p
and
h
.
∎

We introduce the space
\mathscr{C}^{0}_{b}(\mathbb{R}^{+},\mathscr{H}_{r})\cap L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+},\mathscr{H}_{\hbar}^{r})
, and define the sets

\mathscr{E}_{r,\hbar}(\kappa,\delta)=\left\{h\in\mathscr{H}^{r}_{\hbar}\Big|\|h(t=0)\|_{r}\leq\kappa{\delta},\penalty\ \|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r})}+\|h\|_{L^{2}(\mathscr{H}_{\hbar}^{r})}\leq\delta\right\}

\mathscr{E}_{r,\hbar}(\kappa,\delta,h_{0})=\left\{h\in\mathscr{E}_{\hbar}(\delta),\penalty\ h(t=0)=h_{0}\right\}

where

is fix constant depending only on

and
\mathscr{V}
, and
\|h_{0}\|_{r}\leq\kappa_{r}{\delta}
. The constant

will be fixed later.

Proposition 4.11
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
. We define
\mathscr{F}_{\hbar}
the application which associate to
h\in\mathscr{E}_{r,\hbar}
the solution
g
of the equation
3
3

3

The solution always exists as
\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}
and
\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(\cdot,h(t))
are both bounded in
L^{2}(\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v)\to L^{2}(\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v)
.

(4.54)

\left\{\begin{split}\partial_{t}g(t)+\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}g(t)&=\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g(t),h(t)),\\
g(t=0)&=h(t=0).\end{split}\right.

Then for
r>5
, there exist three constants
\kappa_{r}\in(0,1)
,
\delta_{0}>0
,
\hbar_{0}>0
such that for
\kappa\in(0,\kappa_{r})
,

, and

•

the sets

and

,

•

the application

is continuous on
\mathscr{E}_{r,\hbar}(\kappa,\delta)
and contractive on
\mathscr{E}_{r,\hbar}(\kappa,\delta,h_{0})
.

Proof.

Step
1.
The stability of
\mathscr{E}(\delta)
.

Fix
h\in\mathscr{E}_{r,\hbar}(\kappa,\delta)
and
g:=\mathscr{F}_{\hbar}(g)
. We have the following energy inequality.

\displaystyle\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}t}\big\|\big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t)\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\big\langle\big(-\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}\big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t),-\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}g(t)+\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g(t),h(t))\big\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-2\big\langle\big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t)\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}\big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t)\big\rangle+C\|g(t)\|_{\hbar,r^{\prime}-1}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\big(1+\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\big)^{r^{\prime}+1}\big(\|g(t)\|_{\hbar,r^{\prime}}\|h(t)\|_{r^{\prime}-1}+\|g(t)\|_{r^{\prime}-1}\|h(t)\|_{\hbar,r^{\prime}}\big)\|\big\|g(t)\big\|_{\hbar,r}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{C}\big\|\big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t)\big\|^{2}_{\hbar}+C\|g(t)\|_{r^{\prime}-1}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\big(1+\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\big)^{r^{\prime}+1}\big(\|g(t)\|_{\hbar,r^{\prime}}\|h(t)\|_{r^{\prime}-1}+\|g(t)\|_{r^{\prime}-1}\|h(t)\|_{\hbar,r^{\prime}}\big)\|\big\|g(t)\big\|_{\hbar,r}

where we use that
\pi_{0}(g(t))=0
, and Propositions
4.6
and
4.8
.

We deduce that

\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}t}\big\|\big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t)\big\|^{2}+\big(\frac{1}{C}-C\big(1+\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\big)^{r+1}\big(\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}+\|g(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\big)\big)\big\|\big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t)\big\|_{\hbar}^{2}\\
\leq C\big(1+\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\big)^{r^{\prime}+1}\|g(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\big\|\big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\big)^{r^{\prime}}h(t)\big\|^{2}_{\hbar}+C\|g(t)\|_{\hbar,r^{\prime}-1}^{2}

Summing on
r^{\prime}
,

\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{r^{\prime}=0}^{r}\tfrac{2^{r^{\prime}}}{C^{r^{\prime}}}\Big\|\Big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\Big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t)\Big\|^{2}+\Big(\tfrac{1}{2C}-C\Big(1+\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\Big)^{r+1}\Big(\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}+\|g(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\Big)\Big)\sum_{r^{\prime}=0}^{r}\tfrac{2^{r^{\prime}}}{C^{r^{\prime}}}\Big\|\Big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\Big)^{r^{\prime}}g(t)\Big\|_{\hbar}^{2}\\
\leq C\Big(1+\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\Big)^{r^{\prime}+1}\|g(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\sum_{r^{\prime}=0}^{r}\tfrac{2^{r^{\prime}}}{C^{r^{\prime}}}\Big\|\Big(\nabla-\tfrac{v}{2}\Big)^{r^{\prime}}h(t)\Big\|^{2}_{\hbar}

Let
T>0
such that for all
t\in[0,T)
,
\|h(t)\|_{r}\leq\delta
. One can integrate the previous inequality: for
t\in[0,T)

\|g(t)\|^{2}_{r}+\left(\frac{1}{2C}-2C2^{r+1}\delta\right)\int_{0}^{t}\|g(s)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\leq\frac{C^{r}}{2^{r}}\left(\|g(0)\|_{r}^{2}+C2^{r+1}\delta\int_{0}^{t}\|g(s)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\right)

Finally, for
\delta<\delta_{0}:=(4C^{2r+2})^{-1}
,
\kappa_{r}=C^{-(r+1)/2}
, we obtain

\|g(t)\|^{2}_{r}+\frac{1}{4C}\int_{0}^{t}\|g(s)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}\leq\frac{1}{4C}\delta^{2}

By a boot-strap argument, we deduce that
g\in\mathscr{E}_{\hbar}(\delta)
.

Step
2.
Continuity in time of
g
:

As
\|g(t)\|_{r,\hbar}
is a
L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})
, it is bounded for almost all
t^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
.

\displaystyle\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}t}\bigg\|\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\left(g(t)-g(t^{\prime})\right)\bigg\|^{2}\leq-2\int\left(-\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(g(t)-g(t^{\prime})\right)\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}\left(g(t)-g(t^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle-2\int\left(-\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(g(t)-g(t^{\prime})\right)\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}g(t^{\prime})

\displaystyle+C\left(1+\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}-1}\right)^{r^{\prime}+1}\left(\|g(t)\|_{\hbar,r^{\prime}}\|h(t)\|_{r^{\prime}-1}+\|g(t)\|_{r^{\prime}-1}\|h(t)\|_{\hbar,r^{\prime}}\right)\|\left\|g(t)-g(t^{\prime})\right\|_{\hbar,r}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2C}\left\|\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(g(t)-g(t^{\prime})\right)\right\|^{2}_{\hbar}+4C^{2}\|g(t^{\prime})\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+C\|g(t)-g(t^{\prime})\|^{2}_{\hbar,r^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle+\tilde{C}\delta^{2}\left(\|h(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}+\|h(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\right)

Summing on
r^{\prime}\in[0,r]
, we get

\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}t}\left\|g(t)-g(t^{\prime})\right\|_{r}^{2}\lesssim\|g(t^{\prime})\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+\delta^{2}\left(\|h(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}+\|h(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\right).

We deduce that
g
is continuous for almost all
t^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
, and so on
\mathbb{R}^{+}
.

Step
3.
Continuity and contractility of
\mathscr{F}_{\hbar}
.

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\bigg\|\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\left(g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\right)\bigg\|^{2}=-2\int\left(-\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\right)\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}\left(g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\int\left(-\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\right)\left(\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g(t),h(t))-\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(\tilde{g}(t),\tilde{h}(t))\right)

\displaystyle\leq-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C}\left\|\left(\nabla-\frac{v}{2}\right)^{r^{\prime}}\left(g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\right)\right\|_{\hbar}+C\left\|\left(g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\right)\right\|^{2}_{\hbar,r^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(1+\|h(t)\|_{\tilde{r}}+\|\tilde{h}(t)\|_{\tilde{r}}\right)^{r+1}\Big(\left(\|h\|_{\tilde{r}}+\|\tilde{h}\|_{\tilde{r}}\right)\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\|_{\hbar,r}+\|g-\tilde{g}\|_{\tilde{r}}\left(\|h\|_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{h}\|_{\hbar,r}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left(\|g\|_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{g}\|_{\hbar,r}\right)\|h(t)-\tilde{h}(t)\|_{\tilde{r}}+\left(\|g\|_{\tilde{r}}+\|\tilde{g}\|_{\tilde{r}}\right)\|h(t)-\tilde{h}(t)\|_{\hbar,r}\Big)\left\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\right\|_{\hbar,r}.

Summing on
r^{\prime}\in[0,r]
and using the polarization inequalities, and that
g,\tilde{g},h,\tilde{h}\in\mathscr{E}_{\hbar}(\delta)

\frac{d}{dt}\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\|_{r}^{2}+\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\lesssim_{r}\left(\|h(t)\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{h}(t)\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}\right)\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\|_{r}^{2}+\delta^{2}\|h(t)-\tilde{h}(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\\
+\left(\|g\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{g}\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}\right)\|h(t)-\tilde{h}(t)\|^{2}_{{r}}

We begin by treat the part
\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\|_{r}^{2}
. Using the Gronwall’s inequalities,

\displaystyle\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\|_{r}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|g(0)-\tilde{g}(0)\|_{r}^{2}e^{C\int_{0}^{t}\|h\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{h}\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}2e^{C\int_{s}^{t}\|h\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{h}\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}}\left(\delta^{2}\|h(s)-\tilde{h}(s)\|^{2}_{{\hbar,r}}+\left(\|g(s)\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{g}(s)\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}\right)\|h(s)-\tilde{h}(s)\|^{2}_{{r}}\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|h(0)-\tilde{h}(0)\|_{r}^{2}+C\delta^{2}\left[\|h-\tilde{h}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r})}+\|h-\tilde{h}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r}_{\hbar})}\right].

One get now the estimation of
\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}
:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\|g(t)-\tilde{g}(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\infty}\left[\left(\|h(t)\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{h}(t)\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}\right)\left(\|h(0)-\tilde{h}(0)\|_{r}^{2}+\delta^{2}\|h-\tilde{h}\|_{\hbar,rL^{2}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r}_{\hbar})\cap L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r})}^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 40.0pt\left.+\delta^{2}\|h(t)-\tilde{h}(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}+\left(\|g\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}+\|\tilde{g}\|^{2}_{\hbar,r}\right)\|h(t)-\tilde{h}(t)\|^{2}_{{r}}\right]\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{2}\left[\|h-\tilde{h}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r})}+\|h-\tilde{h}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r}_{\hbar})}\right].

We conclude that

\|g-\tilde{g}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r}_{\hbar})\cap L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r})}\lesssim\|h(0)-\tilde{h}(0)\|_{r}+\delta\|h-\tilde{h}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r}_{\hbar})\cap L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{r})}.

This conclude the proof.
∎

We can now proof Theorem
2
: if
\|g_{0}\|<\kappa_{r}\delta
, one can perform a Picard’s fix point argument in
\mathscr{E}_{\hbar}(\kappa,\delta,h_{0})
. In addition,
M(v)+\sqrt{M}(v)g(t,v)
is non-negative for any
(t,v)
by a maximum principle on the equation (
QLB
).

4.5.
Semi-classical (or grazing collision) limit for perturbative solution

We recall that for
g,h
two test functions, and
H:=M+\sqrt{M}h
,

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{0}g(v_{1}):=\left(\nabla-\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)\cdot\int B(M,v_{1},v_{2})\left[\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(\nabla_{1}+\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)g_{1}-\sqrt{M_{1}}\left(\nabla_{2}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right)g_{2}\right]\sqrt{M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{Q}_{0}(g,h)&(v_{1}):=-\left(\nabla-\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)\cdot\int B(H,v_{1},v_{2})\left[h_{2}\nabla g_{1}+g_{2}\nabla h_{1}-h_{1}\nabla g_{2}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right]\sqrt{M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\\
+&\left(\nabla-\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)\cdot\int\left(B(H,v_{1},v_{2})-B(M,v_{1},v_{2})\right)\left[\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(\nabla_{1}+\frac{v_{1}}{2}\right)g_{1}-\sqrt{M_{1}}\left(\nabla_{2}+\frac{v_{2}}{2}\right)g_{2}\right]\sqrt{M_{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\end{split}

\displaystyle B(v_{1},v_{2},H):=\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(H,k,v_{1})|^{2}}k\otimes k\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}

Proposition 4.12
.

We fix the dimension
d\geq 2
. For
f,g
and
h
three test functions,

(4.55)

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle f,\left(\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}-\mathscr{L}_{0}\right)\!g\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\hbar\|f\|_{\hbar}\|g\|_{5}

(4.56)

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle f,\left(\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g,h)-\mathscr{Q}_{0}(g,h)\right)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\hbar\|f\|_{\hbar}\|g\|_{5}\|h\|_{5}(1+\|h\|_{5})^{4}

Proof.

It is sufficient treat for any
f,g,h,p
satisfying

\|g\|_{5}+\|h\|_{5}<\infty,\penalty\ \|p\|_{5}\ll 1,

the operator
\tilde{Q}_{\hbar}(p,h,g)
such that

(4.57)

\tilde{Q}_{\hbar}(p,h,g)(v_{1}):=2\int\left(g_{1}h_{2}-g_{1}^{\prime}h_{2}^{\prime}\right)\frac{\sqrt{M_{2}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F_{p},k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}.

We want to prove that there exists a limiting operator
\tilde{Q}
such that for any test function
f
,

(4.58)

\int f_{1}\tilde{Q}_{\hbar}(p,h,g)(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}=\int f_{1}\tilde{Q}_{0}(p,h,g)_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}+O\left(\hbar\|f\|_{\hbar}(1+\|p\|_{5})^{4}\|h\|_{5}\|g\|_{5}\right).

Step
1.
We begin by the identification of
\tilde{Q}_{0}
.

\int f_{1}\tilde{Q}_{\hbar}(p,h,g)(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}=\int\left(f_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-f_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\left(g_{1}h_{2}-g_{1}^{\prime}h_{2}^{\prime}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F_{p},k,v_{1})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}\\
=\int\left(f_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-f_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\left(h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right)\cdot\frac{k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}+\mathfrak{R}_{1,\hbar}+\int_{0}^{1}\mathfrak{R}_{2,\hbar}^{s}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{s},

where, denoting
v_{1,s}:=v_{1}+s\hbar k
and
v_{2,s}:=v_{2}-s\hbar k
, we define the remaining term as

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{1,\hbar}:=\int\left(f_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-f_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\left(g_{1}h_{2}-g_{1}^{\prime}h_{2}^{\prime}\right)\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}\left(\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F_{p},k,v_{1})|^{2}}-\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},k,v_{1})|^{2}}\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{2,\hbar}^{s}:=(1-s)\int\Big(f_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-f_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\Big)\Big(h_{2,s}\nabla^{2}g_{1,s}-2\nabla g_{1,s}\nabla h_{2,s}+g_{1,s}\nabla^{2}h_{2,s}\Big):\frac{k^{\otimes 2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}.

Using the change of variable
(v_{1},v_{2})\mapsto(v_{1},v_{2}^{\prime})
and
(v_{1},v_{2})\mapsto(v_{1}^{\prime},v_{2})

\displaystyle\int\left(f_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-f_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\left(h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right)\cdot\frac{k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=\int f_{1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{M^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\left(h^{\prime\prime}_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h^{\prime\prime}_{2}\right)}{\hbar|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v^{\prime\prime}_{2})|^{2}}-\frac{\sqrt{M^{\prime}_{2}}\left(h_{2}\nabla g^{\prime\prime}_{1}-g^{\prime\prime}_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right)}{\hbar|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\right)\cdot k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=\int f_{1}k\cdot\left(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2}-\tfrac{v_{2}}{2}\right)\frac{\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right)}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\cdot k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}+\int_{0}^{1}\mathfrak{R}^{s}_{3,\hbar},

where

\mathfrak{R}^{s}_{3,\hbar}:=(1-s)\int f_{1}\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}^{2}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{M_{2,(1-2s)}}\left(h_{2,-s}\nabla g_{1,-s}-g_{1,-s}\nabla h_{2,-s}\right)}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2,-s})|^{2}}\bigg)\cdot k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}.

Finally, using that
k\cdot v_{1}=k\cdot v_{2}
,

\displaystyle\int f_{1}k\cdot\left(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2}-\tfrac{v_{2}}{2}\right)\frac{\sqrt{M_{2}}\left(h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right)}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\cdot k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\tfrac{f_{1}}{\sqrt{M_{1}}}k\cdot\left(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2}\right)\bigg[\frac{\sqrt{M_{2}M_{1}}\left(h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right)}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\cdot k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\bigg]{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int f_{1}M_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{1}\cdot\bigg(\int\left(h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right)\frac{k\otimes k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\sqrt{M_{2}M_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\bigg)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{1}},

where we use that
\left(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2}\right)\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}=0
. We deduce that

\tilde{Q}_{0}(p,h,g)_{1}=M_{1}^{-1/2}\nabla_{1}\cdot\bigg(\int\left(h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}\right)\frac{k\otimes k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},-k,v_{2})|^{2}}\sqrt{M_{2}M_{1}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}{k}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{2}}\bigg).

Step
2.
We estimate now the remaining parts
\mathfrak{R}_{1,\hbar}
,
\mathfrak{R}^{s}_{2,\hbar}
and
\mathfrak{R}^{s}_{3,\hbar}
. Using the orthogonal decomposition
v=v^{\shortparallel}+v^{\perp}
with
v^{\shortparallel}\in\mathbb{R}\hat{k}
and Proposition
3.1
and
4.9
, we obtain that

(4.59)

\displaystyle\int\Big|h_{2,s}\nabla^{2}g_{1,s}-2\nabla g_{1,s}\nabla h_{2,s}+g_{1,s}

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}h_{2,s}\Big|^{2}|k|^{4}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\int|k|^{3}|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}\|g\|^{2}_{2}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(H^{2}_{\perp})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k\lesssim O(\|g\|_{3}^{2}\|h\|_{3}^{2}),

(4.60)

\displaystyle\int\left(f_{1}\sqrt{M_{2}}-f_{1}^{\prime}\sqrt{M_{2}^{\prime}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\lesssim\|f\|_{\hbar}^{2},

(4.61)

\displaystyle\int\left(g_{1}h_{2}-g_{1}^{\prime}h_{2}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\bigg(\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(F_{p},k,v_{1})|^{2}}-

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{0}(F_{p},k,v_{1})|^{2}}\bigg)^{2}\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\lesssim\hbar\int|k||{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)|^{2}

\displaystyle\|g\|^{2}_{1}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(H^{1}_{\perp})}(1+\|p\|_{2})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k\lesssim O(\hbar\|g\|_{3}^{2}\|h\|_{3}^{2}(1+\|p\|_{2})).

Using Cauchy–Schwartz inequality, we deduce that
\mathfrak{R}_{1,\hbar}+\mathfrak{R}^{s}_{2,\hbar}=O(\hbar\|f\|_{\hbar}\|g\|_{3}\|h\|_{3}(1+\|p\|_{2}))
. The third error term can be treated in the same way, and
\mathfrak{R}^{s}_{3,\hbar}=O(\hbar\|f\|_{\hbar}\|g\|_{4}\|h\|_{4}(1+\|p\|_{4}))
. This conclude the proof.
∎

Proof of Theorem
2
.

First, as
\|g_{0}\|_{r}<\delta
and
\hbar<\hbar_{0}
, one can applied Theorem
2
, and for any
\hbar<\hbar_{0}
, there exists
g_{\hbar}
solution of (
QLBℏ-bis
). In addition one have the following bound:

\sup_{t\geq 0}\|g_{\hbar}(t)\|_{r}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\infty}\|g_{\hbar}(t)\|_{\hbar,r}^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}\lesssim\delta^{2}.

We deduce that the family
(g_{\hbar})_{\hbar<\hbar_{0}}
is weakly compact in
\mathscr{C}^{0}_{b}(\mathbb{R}^{+},\mathscr{H}^{r})\cap L^{2}(\mathscr{H}_{\hbar}^{r})
for all
\hbar>0
. Hence, up to the extraction of a subsequence
\hbar^{\prime}
,
(g_{\hbar^{\prime}})
converges weakly to some function
g_{\infty}\in\mathscr{C}^{0}_{b}(\mathbb{R}^{+},\mathscr{H}^{r})\cap L^{2}(\mathscr{H}_{0}^{r})
.

We need to identify the limit. In
[
DW23
]
, the authors show the existence of
g_{\infty}\in\mathscr{C}^{0}_{b}(\mathbb{R}^{+},\mathscr{H}^{r})\cap L^{2}(\mathscr{H}_{0}^{r})
, the unique solution of (
CLB
)-equation, with initial data
g_{\infty}(t=0):=g_{0}
4
4

4

In reality, their results is a little bit weaker, but one can easily adapt their proof.
.

We perform again an energy estimation:

\displaystyle\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}}\|g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)\|^{2}=

\displaystyle-2\big\langle g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t),\mathscr{L}_{\hbar}(g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t))\big\rangle-2\big\langle g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t),(\mathscr{L}_{0}-\mathscr{L}_{\hbar})g_{\infty}(t)\big\rangle

\displaystyle+2\big\langle g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t),\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g_{\hbar}(t),g_{\hbar}(t))-\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g_{\infty}(t),g_{\infty}(t))\big\rangle

\displaystyle+2\big\langle g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t),\mathscr{Q}_{\hbar}(g_{\infty}(t),g_{\infty}(t))-\mathscr{Q}_{\infty}(g_{\infty}(t),g_{\infty}(t))\big\rangle.

Using Proposition
4.6
,
4.9
and
4.12
, and using the same strategy than in the same way than in the proof of Proposition
4.11
,

\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}}\|g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)\|^{2}+(1-\delta)\|g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)\|^{2}_{\hbar}\lesssim\hbar\|g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)\|_{\hbar}\|g_{\infty}\|_{5}\left(1+\|g_{\infty}\|_{5}\right)^{4}.

Hence

\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}}\|g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)\|^{2}+(1-2\delta)\|g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)\|^{2}_{\hbar}\lesssim\frac{\hbar^{2}}{\delta}\|g_{\infty}\|^{2}_{5}\left(1+\|g_{\infty}\|_{5}\right)^{8}.

As
r\geq 5
, we can applied the Gronwall inequality, and obtain

\|g_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)\|\leq\hbar t\|g_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{5}_{\hbar})\cap L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{5})}(1+\|g_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{5}_{\hbar})\cap L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathscr{H}^{5})})^{4}

This conclude the proof. ∎

5.
non-pertubative solutions of the Quantum Lenard–Balescu equation

5.1.
Preliminary

In order to simplify the presentation, we we set the following definition:

Definition 5.1
.

•

Fix
r\in\mathbb{N}
and
\hbar\geq 0
. We define
\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{r}(\mathbb{C})
(respectively
\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{r}(\mathbb{R})
) the set of function
\varpi:\mathbb{R}_{k}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}_{v}\to\mathbb{C}
such that,

(5.1)

\begin{array}[]{c}k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)=0\Rightarrow\varpi(k,v_{1})=\overline{\varpi(-k,v_{2})}\\
\varpi(k,v_{1})=\overline{\varpi(-k,v_{1}+\hbar k}).\end{array}

and such that

(5.2)

\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{r}}=\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j+\ell\leq r,j\leq 2,\ell\leq 1\end{subarray}}\|\tfrac{1}{\langle v_{1}\rangle^{j}}\nabla_{v_{1}}^{i}\nabla_{\hat{k}}^{j}\nabla^{\ell}_{|k|}\varpi\|_{L^{\infty}}

is finite.

•

For
g,h
two functions
\mathbb{R}_{v}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
,
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{0}(\mathbb{R})
, we define

(5.3)

Q_{\hbar}(f,g,\varpi)(v_{1}):=\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left(f_{1}^{\prime}g_{2}^{\prime}-f_{1}g_{2}\right){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}.

•

For any function
p\in L^{2}(\langle v\rangle^{d}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v)
, we recall the definition

(5.4)

\mathbf{H}_{k}(p)[v_{1}]:=\int\frac{p(v_{3})-p(v_{3}-k)}{k\cdot(v_{3}-v_{1}-k)+i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{v_{3}}.

•

We denote
L_{\shortparallel}^{q}(L_{\perp}^{p}):=L^{q}(\mathbb{R}\hat{k},L^{p}(<k>^{\perp}))
.

The Quantum Lenard-Balescu equation can be written as (we linearize around the initial data)

(5.5)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi_{\hbar}=Q_{\hbar}\left(\varphi_{\hbar}(t),\varphi_{\hbar}(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t))|^{2}}\right),\varphi_{\hbar}(t=0)=\Phi_{0},

(5.6)

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi,k,v_{1})=1+{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k){\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(\varphi)(v_{1}).

One can easily check that if
\varphi
is smooth enough,
\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi)
is in
\mathscr{B}^{0}(\mathbb{C})
(as the conditions (
5.1
) are verified).

Control of the discrete derivative

As in the perturbative case, the collision operator allows to control an additional discrete derivative term, given by the following quantity: denoting
L_{\shortparallel}^{q}(L_{\perp}^{p}):=L^{q}(\mathbb{R}\hat{k},L^{p}(<k>^{\perp}))

Definition 5.2
.

We introduce the energy term

(5.7)

A_{\hbar}(f)=\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})^{2}\Phi_{0}(v_{2})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

One can easy show that this is bound by a Sobolev norm:

Lemma 5.1
.

For any mesurable function
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
,

(5.8)

\displaystyle A_{\hbar}(f)\lesssim\|f\|_{H^{1}}^{2},\penalty\ A_{\hbar}(f)\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}.

We begin by proving this result of equivalence of some norms:

Proposition 5.2
.

Fix
\Phi_{0}
such that there exists
v_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
M>0
such that
\Phi_{0}>\tfrac{1}{M}\mathbbm{1}_{M|v-v_{0}|<1}
.

Then for any
f,g\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R})
, with
\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}g\|_{H^{1}}
finite,
\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d+3+\delta}{2}}\mathscr{U}(k)\in L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})
,
\delta>0
,
s\in[-1,1]
, we have

(5.9)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\bigg|\int\mathscr{U}(k)^{2}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})^{2}&g_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-s\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\bigg|\\
&\leq\frac{\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}g\|_{H^{1}}\|\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d+3+\delta}{2}}\mathscr{U}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{C_{\Phi_{0}}}\left[\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\frac{(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{d+5}\langle v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k+\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\right],\end{split}

(5.10)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\frac{\mathscr{U}(k)^{2}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+s\hbar|k|\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\leq\frac{\|\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}\mathscr{U}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{C_{\Phi_{0}}}\left[A_{\hbar}(f)+\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\right]

Proof.

We begin by integrating with respect to
v_{2}
:

\bigg|\int g_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-s\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}\bigg|\lesssim\left(\int\tfrac{\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-s\hbar k)}}{\langle v_{2}\rangle^{d+8}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{2}_{\perp})}\lesssim\tfrac{\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}g\|_{H^{1}}}{\langle v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+s\hbar|k|\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}},

where we use the trace inequality. Hence, (
5.9
) is equivalent to (
5.10
).

Then, using that for
|\hbar k|<1
,
\langle v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+s\hbar k\rangle\geq\langle v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}\rangle
, we obtain that

\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int\frac{\mathscr{U}(k)^{2}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+s\hbar|k|\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int_{\hbar|k|\leq 1}\frac{\mathscr{U}(k)^{2}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})^{2}}{|k|\langle v_{1}\cdot\hat{k}+s\hbar|k|\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}k+\int_{\hbar|k|>1}\frac{\mathscr{U}(k)^{2}}{\hbar^{2}|k|}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\penalty\ \|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using Proposition
4.1
and the bound on
\mathscr{U}
, one can conclude.
∎

Basic integral bounds

The following lemma will be used to bound integrals that often appear in this section.

Proposition 5.3
.

Fix
(s_{j})_{j\leq 3}\in[-1,1]^{3}
,
f,g,h,p
four measurable functions, denoting
(v_{1}^{s},v_{2}^{s}):=(v_{1}+s\hbar k,v_{2}-s\hbar k)
,

(5.11)

\displaystyle\int f(v_{1,s_{1}})g(v_{1,s_{2}})h(v_{2,s_{3}})\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}

\displaystyle\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}}\min(\|g\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}h\|_{H_{1}},\|g\|_{H^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}h\|_{L^{2}}),

(5.12)

\displaystyle\int f(v_{1,s_{1}})g(v_{1,s_{2}})h(v_{2,s_{3}})\mathbf{H}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})|k|^{r}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}

\displaystyle\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\|g\|_{H^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{1}}(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p)+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p\|_{L^{2}}),

(5.13)

\displaystyle\int(f(v_{1})-f(v_{1}^{\prime}))g(v_{1,s_{2}})h(v_{2,s_{3}})\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}

\displaystyle\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\lesssim(A_{\hbar}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|f\|_{L^{2}})\|g\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{1}}.

Proof.

Step
1.
Proof of (
5.11
).

Using a Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,

\int f(v_{1,s_{1}})g(v_{1,s_{2}})h(v_{2,s_{3}})\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
\lesssim\left(\int\tfrac{f(v_{1,s_{1}})^{2}}{\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d}\langle k\rangle^{d+2\eta}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\frac{g(v_{1}^{s_{2}})^{2}(\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d/2}h(v_{2,s_{3}}))^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

Integrating first with respect to
v_{2}
and then to
k
,

\displaystyle\left(\int\tfrac{f(v_{1,s_{1}})^{2}}{\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d}\langle k\rangle^{d+2\eta}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\left(\int\tfrac{f(v_{1,s_{1}})^{2}}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{d+2\eta}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}}.

In the same way, using Assumption
3

\displaystyle\left(\int\frac{g(v_{1}^{s_{2}})^{2}(\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d}h(v_{2,s_{3}}))^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\left(\int\frac{g(v_{1}^{s_{2}})^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{2}_{\perp})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|g\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}h\|_{H_{1}},

where use the trace inequality on
h
. We can obtain the second estimation by integrating first with respect to
v_{1}
and then with respect to
k,v_{2}
.

Step
2.
Proof of (
5.12
).

We perform the same decomposition, one only need to bound

\int\frac{g(v_{1}^{s_{2}})^{2}(\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d/2}h(v_{2,s_{3}}))^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

Using the
L^{2}
bound of the Hilbert transform and integrating with respect to
v_{1}^{\perp}
and
v_{2}^{\perp}
, denoting
v_{3}:=v^{\perp}_{3}+v^{\shortparallel}

\displaystyle\int

\displaystyle\frac{g(v_{1}^{s_{2}})^{2}(\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d/2}h(v_{2,s_{3}}))^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\int\frac{1}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}\|g\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{2}_{\perp})}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{2}_{\perp})}\left(\int\tfrac{p(v_{3})-p(v_{3}+\hbar k)}{\hbar}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{3}^{\perp}\right)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v^{\shortparallel}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|g\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}h\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\int\frac{\langle v_{3}\rangle^{d}(p(v_{3})-p(v_{3}+\hbar k))^{2}}{\hbar^{2}|k|\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{3}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|g\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}h\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\int\frac{(\langle v_{3}\rangle^{\frac{d+5}{2}}p(v_{3})-\langle v^{\prime}_{3}\rangle^{\frac{d+5}{2}}p(v_{3}^{\prime}))^{2}+(\langle v_{3}\rangle^{\frac{d+5}{2}}-\langle v^{\prime}_{3}\rangle^{\frac{d+5}{2}})^{2}p(v_{3}^{\prime})^{2}}{\hbar^{2}|k|\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}\langle v_{3}\rangle^{5}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{3}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Using that
(\langle v_{3}\rangle^{\frac{d+5}{2}}-\langle v^{\prime}_{3}\rangle^{\frac{d+5}{2}})^{2}\lesssim|\hbar k|^{2}(\langle v^{\prime}_{3}\rangle^{d+3}+\langle\hbar k\rangle^{d+3})
and using Proposition
5.2
,

\displaystyle\int

\displaystyle\frac{g(v_{1}^{s_{2}})^{2}(\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d/2}h(v_{2,s_{3}}))^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\lesssim\|g\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}h\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\big(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d+5}{2}}p)+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d+5}{2}}p\|_{L^{2}}\big).

Step
3.
Proof of (
5.13
).

Using a Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,

\int(f(v_{1})-f(v_{1}^{\prime}))g(v_{1,s_{2}})h(v_{2,s_{3}})\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
\lesssim\left(\int\tfrac{(f(v_{1})-f(v_{1}^{\prime}))^{2}}{\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d}\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\frac{g(v_{1}^{s_{2}})^{2}(\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d/2}h(v_{2,s_{3}}))^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{d+2\eta}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Integrating first with respect to
v_{2}
and then to
k

\int(f(v_{1})-f(v_{1}^{\prime}))g(v_{1,s_{2}})h(v_{2,s_{3}})\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
\lesssim\left(\int\tfrac{(f(v_{1})-f(v_{1}^{\prime}))^{2}}{\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{d+8}\langle k\rangle^{2d+6}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\frac{g(v_{1}^{s_{2}})^{2}(\langle v_{2,s_{3}}\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h(v_{2,s_{3}}))^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{d+2\eta}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

We can conclude by applying Proposition
5.2
for the right hand side and the same strategy than in the first step for the right hand side.
∎

5.2.
Cancellation lemmas

The main purpose of this section is to take advantages of the cancellation

\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k=C_{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}}\hbar\tfrac{v_{2}-v_{1}}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|^{2}}

for some constant
C_{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}}>0
. This is due to the symmetry
k\mapsto-k
inside the integral

\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k=0.

When we add an asymmetry
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}^{2}(R)
(which will be a derivative of the dielectric constant
\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}|^{2}})
, the symmetry
k\mapsto-k
remains almost true, with
\varpi(-k,v_{1})=\varpi(k,v_{1}-\hbar k)
. Hence,

\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k=\frac{1}{2}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k(\varpi(k,v_{1})-\varpi(k,v_{1}-\hbar k))\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\\
=\frac{\hbar}{2}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\cdot\nabla_{v_{1}}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k+O(\hbar^{2}).

The following lemma exploit this idea.

Proposition 5.4
.

Consider
f,g,h\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, and
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}^{1}(\mathbb{R})
. Then

(5.14)

\frac{1}{\hbar}\int f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}(\|f\|_{L^{2}}+A_{\hbar}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}})\|g\|_{H^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}h\|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

We begin by performing the decomposition

\frac{1}{\hbar}\int f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
=\int\tfrac{f_{1}g_{1}-f_{1}^{\prime}g_{1}^{\prime}}{\hbar}h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V+\frac{1}{\hbar}\int f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}h_{2}\varpi(-k,v_{2}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

Using Proposition
5.3
, the left hand side is bounded by
O(\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}(\|f\|_{L^{2}}+A_{\hbar}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}})\|g\|_{H^{1}}\|h\|_{L^{2}})
.

For the left hand side, using the change of variable
v_{1}\mapsto v_{1}^{\prime}
and the symmetry
k\mapsto-k
,

\frac{1}{\hbar}\int f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}h_{2}\varpi(-k,v_{2}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V=\frac{1}{\hbar}\int f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\varpi(-k,v_{2}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
=\frac{1}{2\hbar}\int f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}(\varpi(-k,v_{2})-\varpi(-k,v_{2}+\hbar k)){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

Using that

\frac{1}{\hbar}\int(\varpi(-k,v_{2})-\varpi(-k,v_{2}+\hbar k)){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k=O\left(\tfrac{\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}\right),

and applying the Hardy-Littelwood and Sobolev inequalities, if
d\geq 3
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{\hbar}\int f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{d-2}}}\|h\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{H^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}h\|_{L^{2}}.

If
d=2
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{\hbar}\int f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\bigg|\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{L^{2}}\|h\|_{L^{2}}.

This conclude the proof.
∎

This proposition is an extension of the previous one in the case where the asymmetric term
{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\varpi(k,v_{1})
is not regular. The idea is to transform the discrete derivative in
v_{1}
that appear in
{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})
into a derivative in
k
(it is similar to the proof of estimation (2.26) of
[
DW23
]
).

Proposition 5.5
.

Fix
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{1}(\mathbb{C})
,
f,g,h,p\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
. Then for any
K\geq 1
,

(5.15)

\left|\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{\hbar}\Re(\varpi(k,v_{1}){\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1}))f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\right|\\
\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\left(K^{4}\|f\|_{L^{2}}+K^{-1}A_{\hbar}(f)\right)\|g\|_{H^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{2}}\left(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p)+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Proof.

Fix
\varphi:\mathbb{R_{+}}\to[0,1]
an increasing function, with
\chi(1)=1
,
\chi(0)=0
and
\chi^{\prime}
is supported in
[1/3,2/3]
.

Using the orthogonal decomposition
v=v^{\shortparallel}+v^{\perp}
with
v^{\shortparallel}:=\hat{k}\cdot v\hat{k}
, we define in the following

{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}^{K}(p)(v_{1}):=\int\chi(K|v_{1}^{\perp}-v_{3}^{\perp}|)\tfrac{p(v_{3}+\hbar k)-p(v_{3})}{\hbar k\cdot(v_{3}-v_{1})+i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{3},\penalty\ \tilde{{\mathbf{H}}}_{\hbar k}^{K}(p)={\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)-{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}^{K}(p).

Step
1.
Treatment of
{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}^{K}
:

Using the change of variable
v_{2}\mapsto v_{2}-\hbar k
, the symmetry
k\mapsto-k
, and that for
k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})=0
,
\varpi(k,v_{1})=\varpi(k,v_{2})
, the first term is equal to

\displaystyle\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{\hbar}\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{1}){\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)f_{1}h_{2}g_{1}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2\hbar}\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{2}){\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})-\varpi(-k,v_{2}){\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{-\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

(5.16)

\displaystyle=\Re\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2\hbar}\big(\varpi(k,v_{2})-\varpi(k,v_{2}-\hbar k)\big){\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1}-\hbar k)f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

(5.17)

\displaystyle+\Re\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2\hbar}\varpi(k,v_{2})\big({\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})-{\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1}-\hbar k)\big)f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

Using that
\|\nabla_{v_{1}}\varpi\|_{L^{\infty}}
is bounded, (
5.16
) can be treated by using Proposition
5.3
. The main difficulty is the treatment of (
5.17
). The main idea is to transform the discrete derivative in
v_{1}
of
\big({\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})-{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1}-\hbar k)\big)\big)
into a derivative in
k
and
v_{2}
(via integration by part).

Using that
{\rm div}_{v}\tfrac{v}{|v|^{2}}=\tfrac{d-2}{|v|^{2}}
and that
\nabla_{k}|v^{\perp}|=-\tfrac{\hat{k}\cdot v}{|k||v^{\perp}|}v^{\perp}
one has

\displaystyle|k|^{2}{\rm div}_{k}\left(\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{k\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\tfrac{v-s\hbar k}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\hbar|k|^{2}\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)(v-2s\hbar k)}{(\hbar k\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0)^{2}}\tfrac{v-s\hbar k}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}+(d-2)\tfrac{\hbar|k|^{2}\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hbar k\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\tfrac{-s\hbar}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}+\tfrac{|k|^{2}}{k\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\tfrac{v-s\hbar k}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}\cdot v^{\perp}\tfrac{K\chi^{\prime}(K|v^{\perp}|)\hat{k}\cdot v}{|k||v^{\perp}|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\hbar^{2}|k|^{2}\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{(\hbar k\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0)^{2}}-(d-3)\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-\hbar k)+i0}\tfrac{s\hbar|k|}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}-\tfrac{(\hat{k}\cdot v)K\chi^{\prime}(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\tfrac{|v^{\perp}|}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s}\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hbar k\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}-\tfrac{(d-3)\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)+K\chi^{\prime}(K|v^{\perp}|)|v^{\perp}|}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-\hbar k)+i0}\tfrac{s\hbar|k|}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}-\tfrac{K\chi^{\prime}(K|v^{\perp}|)|v^{\perp}|}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}.

Denoting

(5.18)

\displaystyle\psi_{\hbar k}^{1}(v)=\int_{0}^{1}\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\tfrac{v-s\hbar k}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s

(5.19)

\displaystyle\psi_{\hbar k}^{2}(v)=\int_{0}^{1}\tfrac{K\chi^{\prime}(K|v^{\perp}|)|v^{\perp}|}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s

(5.20)

\displaystyle\psi_{\hbar k}^{3}(v)=\int_{0}^{1}\left(\tfrac{s((d-3)\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)+K\chi^{\prime}(K|v^{\perp}|)|v^{\perp}|)}{(\hat{k}\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0)|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}-\tfrac{(s-1)((d-3)\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)+K\chi^{\prime}(K|v^{\perp}|)|v^{\perp}|)}{(\hat{k}\cdot(v-(s-1)\hbar k)+i0)|v-(s-1)\hbar k|^{2}}\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s,

we deduce that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})-{\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1}-\hbar k)\big)=\int p(v_{1}-v)\Big(\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hbar k\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}-\tfrac{2\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hbar k\cdot v+i0}+\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hbar k\cdot(v+s\hbar k)+i0}\Big)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\int p(v_{1}-v)\int_{0}^{1}\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s}\left(\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hbar k\cdot(v-(s-1)\hbar k)+i0}-\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hbar k\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\right)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-|k|^{2}{\rm div}_{k}\left[\tfrac{1}{|k|}\psi^{1}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}(p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k))\right]\!(v_{1})-\left[\psi^{2}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}(p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k))\right]\!(v_{1})+\hbar|k|\left[\psi^{3}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}p\right]\!(v_{1})

Hence

\displaystyle\eqref{xi}=

\displaystyle\Re\int\tfrac{|k|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2}\varpi(k,v_{2})f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\left[\psi^{3}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}p\right](v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\Re\int\tfrac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2}\varpi(k,v_{2})f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\left[\psi^{2}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\right](v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\Re\int\tfrac{1}{|k|}\nabla_{k}\left[\tfrac{|k|^{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2}\varpi(k,v_{2})f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\right]\cdot\left[\psi^{1}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\right](v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

Using that
\nabla_{k}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}=\left[\tfrac{k}{|k|^{2}}\cdot\nabla_{v_{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\right](v_{2}-v_{1})
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\Re\int\tfrac{1}{|k|}\nabla_{k}\left[\tfrac{|k|^{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2}\varpi(k,v_{2})f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\right]\cdot\left[\psi^{1}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\right](v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}

\displaystyle=\Re\int\tfrac{1}{|k|}\nabla_{k}\cdot\left[\tfrac{|k|^{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2}\varpi(k,v_{2})\right]f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\big[\psi^{1}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\big](v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle-\Re\int\tfrac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2|k|}f_{1}g_{1}k\cdot\nabla_{v_{2}}\left[h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{2})\right](v_{2}-v_{1})\cdot\big[\psi^{1}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\big](v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

As we do not want any new weight in
v_{1}
, we have to care about the new factor
v_{1}
that appears in the last line:

\displaystyle v_{1}\cdot\big[\psi^{1}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\big](v_{1})

\displaystyle=\int\tfrac{p(v_{1}-v)-p(v_{1}-v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}(v_{1}-v)\cdot\int_{0}^{1}\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\tfrac{v-s\hbar k}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle+\int\tfrac{p(v_{1}-v)-p(v_{1}-v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\int_{0}^{1}\underset{=\frac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-s\hbar k)}+\frac{s\hbar\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)v}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\cdot\tfrac{v-s\hbar k}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\psi^{1}_{\hbar k}\dot{\ast}_{v}\Big(

\displaystyle\tfrac{p(v)-p(v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}v\Big)(v_{1})+\psi^{4}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\Big(\tfrac{p(v)-p(v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\Big)(v_{1})+|k|\psi^{5}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\Big(\tfrac{p(v)-p(v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\Big)(v_{1})

where we have denoted
\dot{\ast}
convolution product with scalar product (
f\dot{\ast}g(v):=\int f(v_{*})\cdot g(v-v_{*})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{*}
), and

(5.21)

\displaystyle\psi^{4}_{\hbar k}(v):=\int_{0}^{1}\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot(v-s\hbar k)+i0}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s,\qquad\psi^{5}_{\hbar k}(v):=\int_{0}^{1}\tfrac{s\hbar\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{|v-s\hbar k|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s.

Integrating with respect to
v_{2}
and using the bound on
\varpi
and
{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}
, (
5.17
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\int\tfrac{1}{\langle k\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}d+2+\eta}}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{1}_{\perp})}\int\left|f(v_{1})\right|\left|g(v_{1})\right|\left|\psi^{3}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}p\right|(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle+\int\tfrac{1}{\langle k\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}d+3+\eta}}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle\langle\nabla\rangle h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{1}_{\perp})}\int\left|f(v_{1})\right|\left|g(v_{1})\right|\sum_{j\in\{1,2,4,5\}}\left|\psi^{j}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\big(\tfrac{p(v)-p(v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\big)\right|(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle+\int\tfrac{1}{\langle k\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}d+3+\eta}}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle\langle\nabla\rangle h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{1}_{\perp})}\int\left|f(v_{1})\right|\left|g(v_{1})\right|\left|\psi^{1}_{\hbar k}\dot{\ast}_{v}\big(\tfrac{p(v)-p(v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}v\big)\right|(v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

The terms
\psi_{\hbar k}^{i}
are controlled by the following lemma:

Lemma 5.6
.

Fix
k\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. There exists a constant
C>0
such that for any function
f
and any
K>1
, for any
d\geq 2
, one has

(5.22)

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot v+i0}\ast f\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\lesssim\ \left\|f\right\|_{L^{1}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})},\penalty\ \big\|\tfrac{1-\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot v+i0}\ast f\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\lesssim K^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}},

for any
d\geq 3
, for any
p\in[1,\tfrac{d-1}{d-2}]
and
q=\tfrac{p(d-1)}{d-1-(d-2)p}
,

(5.23)

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}+\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)v}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}+\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)|v^{\perp}|}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\leq C\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})},

for any
d\geq 4
, for any
p\in[1,\tfrac{d-1}{d-3}]
and
q=\tfrac{p(d-1)}{d-1-(d-3)p}
,

(5.24)

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\leq C\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})}

for
d=2
and for any
p,q\in[1,\infty]

(5.25)

\displaystyle\text{if }q<p,\penalty\ \big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}+\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)v}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\leq CK^{\tfrac{1}{q}-\tfrac{1}{p}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})},

(5.26)

\displaystyle\text{if }q<p,\penalty\ \big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\leq CK^{1+\tfrac{1}{q}-\tfrac{1}{p}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})}

We postpone the proof in Appendix
A
.

Using this lemma with the maximal admissible
p\in[1,2]
, and that
\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})}\leq\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}f\|_{L^{2}}
for
p\in[1,2]
, one has

\displaystyle\left\|\psi^{3}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}p\right\|_{L^{q(d)}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})+L^{\infty}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\lesssim K^{2}\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}p\big\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\{1,2,4,5\}}\left\|\chi^{i}_{\hbar k}\ast_{v}\big(\tfrac{p(v)-p(v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\big)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}+\left\|\chi^{1}_{\hbar k}\dot{\ast}_{v}\big(\tfrac{p(v)-p(v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}v\big)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\lesssim K\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}\tfrac{p(v)-p(v-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\big\|_{L^{2}}.

where
q(2)=q(3)=2
,
q(4)=6
and
q(d)=\infty
for
d\geq 5
.

Using Sobolev injections (in the following,
\mathscr{C}^{0}_{\shortparallel}
is the set of function continuous with the norm
L^{\infty}
, which is a separable Banach space),

(5.27)

\displaystyle\forall d\geq 2,\penalty\ \|f\|_{L^{2}_{\perp}(\mathscr{C}^{0}_{\shortparallel})}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}_{\perp}(H^{1}_{\shortparallel})}\leq\|f\|_{H^{1}},

(5.28)

\displaystyle d\leq 3,\penalty\ \|f\|_{\mathscr{C}^{0}}\lesssim\|f\|_{H^{2}},

(5.29)

\displaystyle d=4,\penalty\ \|f\|_{L^{3}_{\perp}(\mathscr{C}_{\shortparallel}^{0})}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{3}_{\perp}(H^{1})}\lesssim\|f\|_{H^{2}},

and trace estimates

(5.30)

\forall d\geq 3,\penalty\ \|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{1}_{\perp})}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d-1}{2}}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{2}_{\perp})}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d-1}{2}}h\|_{H^{1}}.

Applying Hölder inequality, we deduce that

|\eqref{xi}|\lesssim\int\tfrac{K}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}h\|_{H^{2}}\|g\|_{H^{2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}p\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\\
+\int\tfrac{K^{2}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}h\|_{H^{2}}\|g\|_{H^{2}}\|\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

It remains to bound
\int\tfrac{1}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k
. By Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,

\displaystyle\int\tfrac{1}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\lesssim\left(\int\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k}{\langle k\rangle^{d+2\eta}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\tfrac{\langle v_{1}\rangle^{{d+2}}}{\hbar^{2}\langle k\rangle^{3d+4}}(f(v_{1}^{\prime})-f(v_{1}))^{2}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int\tfrac{(\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{\frac{d+3}{2}}f(v_{1}^{\prime})-\langle v_{1}\rangle^{\frac{d+3}{2}}f(v_{1}))^{2}}{\hbar^{2}\langle k\rangle^{3d+4}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{5}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int\tfrac{(\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{\frac{d+3}{2}}-\langle v_{1}\rangle^{\frac{d+3}{2}})^{2}f(v^{\prime}_{1})^{2}}{\hbar^{2}\langle k\rangle^{3d+4}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{5}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

It remains to bound

\tfrac{(\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{\frac{d+3}{2}}-\langle v_{1}\rangle^{\frac{d+3}{2}})^{2}}{\hbar^{2}\langle k\rangle^{3d+4}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{5}}\lesssim\tfrac{|k|^{2}(\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{d+3}+\langle v_{1}\rangle^{d-3})}{\langle k\rangle^{3d+4}}\lesssim\tfrac{\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{d+3}}{\langle k\rangle^{d+2}}+\tfrac{\langle\hbar k\rangle^{d-3}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+2}},

and applying Proposition
5.2
,

(5.31)

\int\tfrac{1}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d+1}{2}}\tfrac{p(\cdot)-p(\cdot-\hbar k)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\lesssim A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p)+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p\|_{L^{2}.}

This concludes the proof that

(5.32)

\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{\hbar}\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{1}){\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)f_{1}h_{2}g_{1}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{H^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}h\|_{H^{2}}(K^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}p\|_{L^{2}}+KA_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+1}p)^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Step
2.
Treatment of
\tilde{{\mathbf{H}}}_{\hbar k}^{K}
:

Using the symmetry
(v_{1},v_{2},k)\mapsto(v_{1}^{\prime},v_{2}^{\prime},-k)
,

\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{\hbar}\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{1}){\mathbf{H}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
=\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{2\hbar}\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{1})\tilde{{\mathbf{H}}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)(f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}-f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}h^{\prime}_{2})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

Using the decomposition

\tfrac{f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}-f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}h^{\prime}_{2}}{\hbar}=\tfrac{f_{1}-f_{1}^{\prime}}{\hbar}g_{1}h_{2}+f^{\prime}_{1}\tfrac{g^{\prime}_{1}-g_{1}}{\hbar}h_{2}+f_{1}^{\prime}g_{1}^{\prime}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar},

using that
\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}=\hat{k}\cdot v_{2}
, denoting
v_{1,s}:=v_{1}+s\hbar k
and integrating with respect to
v_{2}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\int\tfrac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{\hbar}\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{1})\tilde{{\mathbf{H}}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\int\tfrac{\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+1+\eta}\langle\hat{k}\cdot v_{1}\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}}\big(\big|\tfrac{f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1}}{\hbar}\big|+|f^{\prime}_{1}|\big)\big(\big|\nabla g(v_{1,s})\big|+|g_{1}|+|g^{\prime}_{1}|\big)\big|\tilde{{\mathbf{H}}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big|\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}\langle\nabla\rangle h\|_{L^{\infty}_{\shortparallel}(L^{1}_{\perp})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\tfrac{\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\|\langle\nabla\rangle g\|_{L^{2}(\mathscr{C}^{0}_{b})}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{2}}}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}\big(\big\|\tfrac{f(v+\hbar k)-f(v)}{\hbar\langle\hat{k}\cdot v\rangle^{9/4}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+|k|\|f\|_{L^{2}}\big)\|\tfrac{1-\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot v+i0}\ast\tfrac{p(\cdot+\hbar k)-p(\cdot)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\tfrac{\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\|g\|_{H^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{2}}}{K^{\frac{1}{2}}|k|\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}\big(\big\|\tfrac{f(v+\hbar k)-f(v)}{\hbar\langle\hat{k}\cdot v\rangle^{9/4}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+|k|\|f\|_{L^{2}}\big)\|\tfrac{p(\cdot+\hbar k)-p(\cdot)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Using Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,

\displaystyle\int\tfrac{1}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{f(v+\hbar k)-f(v)}{\hbar\langle\hat{k}\cdot v\rangle^{9/4}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\|\tfrac{p(\cdot+\hbar k)-p(\cdot)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int\tfrac{(f(v+\hbar k)-f(v))^{2}}{\hbar|k|\langle k\rangle^{d+3+\eta}\langle\hat{k}\cdot v\rangle^{9/2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\tfrac{(p(v+\hbar k)-p(v))^{2}}{\hbar|k|\langle k\rangle^{3d+1+\eta}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq A_{\hbar}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\tfrac{(\langle v+\hbar k\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}p(v+\hbar k)-\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}p(v))^{2}}{\hbar|k|\langle k\rangle^{3d+1+\eta}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}}+\tfrac{p(v+\hbar k)^{2}(\langle v+\hbar k\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}-\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}})^{2}}{\hbar|k|\langle k\rangle^{3d+1+\eta}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

As
\tfrac{|\langle v+\hbar k\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}-\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}|^{2}}{\hbar|k|\langle k\rangle^{3d+1+\eta}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{2}}}\leq\tfrac{\langle v\rangle^{\frac{5}{2}}}{\langle k\rangle^{3d-5/2}}
and
d\geq 2
,

\int\tfrac{1}{|k|\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}\big\|\tfrac{f(v+\hbar k)-f(v)}{\hbar\langle\hat{k}\cdot v\rangle^{9/4}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\|\tfrac{p(\cdot+\hbar k)-p(\cdot)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\lesssim(A_{\hbar}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|f\|_{L^{2}})(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}p)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}p\|_{L^{2}}),

and in the same way

\displaystyle\int\tfrac{1}{\langle k\rangle^{2d+2+\eta}}

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\tfrac{p(\cdot+\hbar k)-p(\cdot)}{\hbar}\|_{L^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}}(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}p)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}p\|_{L^{2}}).

We deduce that

\bigg|\int\tfrac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}k}{\hbar}\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{1})\tilde{{\mathbf{H}}}^{K}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}\bigg|\\
\lesssim\tfrac{\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\|g\|_{H^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{2}}}{K^{\frac{1}{2}}}(A_{\hbar}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|f\|_{L^{2}})(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}p)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{9}{4}}p\|_{L^{2}}).

∎

The following proposition is the counter part of Lemma 2.6 of
[
HLPZ24
]
, and will be use to transfer regularity from one term to an other.

Proposition 5.7
.

Consider
f,g,h\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, and
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}^{2}(\mathbb{R})
. Then for any
K\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int(f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle-f_{1}g_{1})h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\left((K^{4}\|f\|_{L^{2}}+K^{-1}A_{\hbar}(f)\right)

\displaystyle\times\min\left(\|g\|_{H^{2}}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{L^{2}}+A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right),\left(\|g\|_{L^{2}}+A_{\hbar}(g)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{2}}\right).

Proof.

Fix
\chi:\mathbb{R_{+}}\to[0,1]
an increasing function, with
\chi(1)=1
,
\chi(0)=0
and
\chi^{\prime}
is supported in
[1/3,2/3]
. We perform the decomposition

Using the symmetry
(v_{1},v_{2},k)\mapsto(v_{1}^{\prime},v_{2}^{\prime},-k)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}

\displaystyle(f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}g_{1})h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V=\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

(5.33)

\displaystyle=\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}(1-\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|))\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

(5.34)

\displaystyle+\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

Step
1.
We begin by the bound of (
5.33
). Using Young inequality,

\displaystyle\bigg|\int

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}(1-\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|))\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{K|v_{1}-v_{2}|\leq 1}\big(\big|g_{1}\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}}{\hbar}\big|+\big|f^{\prime}_{1}\tfrac{g^{\prime}_{1}-g_{1}}{\hbar}\big|\big)\big|\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\big|\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\bigg(\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\tfrac{\mathbbm{1}_{K|v^{\perp}_{1}-v^{\perp}_{2}|\leq 1}}{\langle v_{2}\cdot\hat{k}\rangle^{9/2}}\big(\big|\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}}{\hbar}\big|^{2}+|f^{\prime}_{1}|^{2}\big)\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\bigg(\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\langle v_{2}\cdot\hat{k}\rangle^{9/2}\big(\big|\tfrac{g^{\prime}_{1}-g_{1}}{\hbar}\big|^{2}+|g_{1}|^{2}\big)\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}|\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\big|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Using the same strategy than in the proof of Proposition
5.3
,

\bigg|\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}(1-\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|))\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\bigg|\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|f\|_{L^{2}}+A_{\hbar}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}})\\
\times\min\left(\|g\|_{H^{1}}(\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{L^{2}}+A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h)^{\frac{1}{2}}),(\|g\|_{L^{2}}+A_{\hbar}(g)^{\frac{1}{2}})\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{1}}\right).

Step
2.
Estimation of (
5.34
). Denoting
(v_{1}^{\prime\prime}):=v_{1}-\hbar k
, and using that
|v_{1}-v_{2}-\hbar k|=|v_{1}-v_{2}+\hbar k|
if
k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})=0
and
|v_{1}-v_{2}-2\hbar k|=|v_{1}-v_{2}|
if
k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2}+\hbar k)=0
,

(5.35)

\int\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
