=\int\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\left[\tfrac{\varpi(k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})}{\hbar^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}+\hbar k)}-\tfrac{\varpi(k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})+\varpi(k,v_{1})}{\hbar^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}+\tfrac{\varpi(k,v_{1})}{\hbar^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\right]{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
-\int f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\tfrac{\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)-\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}-\hbar k|)}{\hbar^{2}}(\varpi(k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})+\varpi(k,v_{1}))\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

We need the following estimation:

Lemma 5.8
.

Consider
\varpi(k,v_{1})\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{2}(\mathbb{R})
. There exists some constant
C>0
such that

(5.36)

\bigg|\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\left[\tfrac{\varpi(k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})}{\hbar^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}+\hbar k)}-\tfrac{\varpi(k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})+\varpi(k,v_{1})}{\hbar^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}+\tfrac{\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}}{\hbar^{2}}\right]\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\bigg|\\
\leq\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\left(\tfrac{C}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|^{2}}+\tfrac{C}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|^{3}}\right).

Proof.

For
k\in<v_{1}-v_{2}>^{\perp}
, with
|k|\leq\frac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}{\hbar}
we introduce (as in the Step 2 of the proof of Proposition
4.4
)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}k:=\left(\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|})-1\right)k+\frac{\hbar|k|^{2}}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|^{2}}(v_{2}-v_{1}),\penalty\ \Lambda(x):=\mathbbm{1}_{|x|\leq 1}\sqrt{1-x^{2}}.

The application

\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\left\{k,\penalty\ k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})=0,|k|\leq\frac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}{\hbar}\right\}\to\left\{\tilde{k},\penalty\ \tilde{k}\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar\tilde{k})=0\right\},\\
k\mapsto\tilde{k}:=k+\bar{\delta}k\end{array}\right.

is a diffeomorphism with Jacobian

\delta_{\tilde{k}\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar\tilde{k})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{\tilde{k}}=\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|})^{d-3}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k,

and
|k|=|\tilde{k}|
.

We introduce
k_{s}:=|k|(\cos(s\theta_{k})\hat{k}+\sin(s\theta_{k})\widehat{v_{2}-v_{1}})
with
\theta_{k}:=2\arcsin\tfrac{|\delta k|}{2|k|}
.

We deduce that,

\displaystyle\int

\displaystyle\left[\tfrac{\varpi(k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})}{\hbar^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}+\hbar k)}-\tfrac{\varpi(k,v^{\prime\prime}_{1})+\varpi(k,v_{1})}{\hbar^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}+\tfrac{\varpi(k,v_{1})}{\hbar^{2}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\right]{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle=\int\left[\tfrac{\varpi(k_{0},v_{1})+\varpi(k_{0},v_{1}-\hbar k_{0})}{\hbar^{2}}-\tfrac{(\varpi(k_{1},v_{1})+\varpi(k_{-1},v_{1}-\hbar k_{-1})}{\hbar^{2}}\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|})^{d-3}\right]{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

(5.37)

\displaystyle=\int\left[\varpi(k,v_{1})+\varpi(k,v_{1}-\hbar k)\right]\tfrac{1-\Lambda\big(\frac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|}\big)^{d-3}}{\hbar^{2}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

(5.38)

\displaystyle+\int\tfrac{\varpi(k_{0},v_{1})+\varpi(k_{0},v_{1}-\hbar k_{0})-\varpi(k_{1},v_{1})-\varpi(k_{-1},v_{1}-\hbar k_{-1})}{\hbar^{2}}\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|})^{d-3}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

Using the bound on
\varpi
and that
{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}
checks Assumption
3
,

\displaystyle|\eqref{eq:K1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}}{\hbar^{2}}\int\left|\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|})^{d-3}-1\right|\frac{\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}}{|k|^{d}}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}}{\hbar^{2}}\int\frac{\left|\Lambda(|u|)^{d-3}-1\right|}{|u|^{d}}\frac{\hbar}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|}\delta_{\frac{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}{\hbar}u\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|^{3}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\left|\Lambda(|u|)^{d-3}-1\right|}{|u|^{2}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}|u|.

As
d\geq 2
, this is bounded.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\eqref{eq:K2}=\int\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}^{2}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}}\tfrac{\varpi(k_{s-s^{\prime}},v_{1}-s^{\prime}\hbar k_{s-s^{\prime}})}{\hbar^{2}}\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|})^{d-3}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k.

Hence, using that
\theta_{k}\lesssim\tfrac{|\delta k|}{|k|}\lesssim\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}
,

\displaystyle|\eqref{eq:K2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\left(\tfrac{|k|^{2}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}+\tfrac{|k|^{2}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}\right)\Lambda(\tfrac{\hbar|k|}{|v_{2}-v_{1}|})^{d-3}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\left(\tfrac{1}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{3}}\right).

This conclude the proof.
∎

Lemma 5.9
.

There exists some constant
C>0
such that

(5.39)

\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\left|\tfrac{\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)-\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}-\hbar k|)}{\hbar^{2}}\right|\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\leq C\tfrac{K^{2}}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}.

Proof.

We begin by computing the derivative

\displaystyle\nabla\chi(K|v|)=K\chi^{\prime}(K|v|)\tfrac{v}{|v|}

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}\chi(K|v|)=K^{2}\chi^{\prime\prime}(K|v|)\tfrac{v\otimes v}{|v|^{2}}+K\chi^{\prime}(K|v|)\tfrac{\rm Id}{|v|}-K\chi^{\prime}(K|v|)\tfrac{v\otimes v}{|v|^{3}}=O(K^{2}),

where we use that
\chi^{\prime}
is supported in
[1/3,2/3]
.

Hence, applying a Taylor expansion, as
(v_{1}-v_{2})\cdot k=0
,

\displaystyle\tfrac{\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)-\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}-\hbar k|)}{\hbar^{2}}=\int_{0}^{1}(1-s)\nabla^{2}\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}-s\hbar k|):k^{\otimes 2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s=O(K^{2}|k|^{2}).

We conclude by integrating with respect to
k
. ∎

Applying the two previous lemmas, decomposition (
5.35
) and Hardy-Littlewood inequality,

\displaystyle\bigg|\int\chi(K|v_{1}-v_{2}|)\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{2}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\int\tfrac{1}{|v_{1}-v_{2}|}f_{1}g_{1}h_{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{1}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{2}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{L^{2}}\|h\|_{L^{\frac{d}{d-1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{2}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{L^{2}}.

This conclude the proof.
∎

5.3.
Non-Linear estimates

Proposition 5.10
.

Consider
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{0}(\mathbb{R})
,
f,g,h:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
three measurable functions, for any
K>1
,

(5.40)

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi)\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{1}}

(5.41)

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi)\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}A_{\hbar}(f)^{\frac{1}{2}}\min\left(\|g\|_{H_{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{2}},\|g\|_{H_{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{1}}\right)

(5.42)

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi)\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\Big(K^{4}\|f\|_{L^{2}}+K^{-1}A_{\hbar}(f)\Big)\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\Big(\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{L^{2}}+A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\|g\|_{H^{3}}.

Consider
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{1}(\mathbb{C})
,
f,g,h,p:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
three measurable functions, then, for any
K>1
,

(5.43)

\left|\left\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\Re(\varpi{\mathbf{H}}(p))\right\rangle\right|\\
\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\Big(K^{4}\|f\|_{L^{2}}+K^{-1}A_{\hbar}(f)\Big)\|g\|_{H_{3}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{3}}\left(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p)+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Proof.

Step
1.
Proof of (
5.40
). It is a direct application of Proposition
5.3
.

Step
2.
Proof of (
5.41
). Using the symmetry
(v_{1},v_{2},k)\mapsto(v_{1}^{\prime},v_{2}^{\prime},-k))
,

\displaystyle\left\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})g_{1}h_{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})\left[\tfrac{g_{1}+g_{1}^{\prime}}{2}h_{2}+\tfrac{g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime}}{2}h_{2}\right]\varpi(k,v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})\left[\tfrac{g_{1}+g_{1}^{\prime}}{2}\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{2}+\tfrac{g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime}}{2}h_{2}\right]\varpi(k,v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\int\frac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{\hbar}(f_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1})\left[\tfrac{g_{1}+g_{1}^{\prime}}{2}\tfrac{k\cdot\nabla h(v_{2}+(s-1))}{2}-\tfrac{k\cdot\nabla g(v_{1}+s\hbar k)}{2}h_{2}\right]\varpi(k,v_{1})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s.

We conclude by applying Proposition
5.3
.

Step
3.
We treat now (
5.42
):

\displaystyle\big\langle f,Q_{\hbar}

\displaystyle(g,h,\varpi)\big\rangle=\int\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}}{\hbar^{2}}g_{1}h_{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=\int\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar^{2}}h_{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V+\int f^{\prime}_{1}\tfrac{g_{1}-g_{1}^{\prime}}{\hbar^{2}}h_{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

(5.44)

\displaystyle=\int\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}g_{1}^{\prime}-f_{1}g_{1}}{\hbar^{2}}h_{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V+\tfrac{1}{\hbar}\int h_{2}f^{\prime}_{1}\nabla g^{\prime}_{1}\cdot k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

(5.45)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}s\int h_{2}f^{\prime}_{1}\nabla^{2}g(v_{1}+s\hbar k):k^{\otimes 2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

The term (
5.44
) is bounded by Propositions
5.7
and
5.4
, and the term (
5.45
) is treated by applying Proposition
5.3
.

Step
4.
Proof of (
5.43
). Applying a Taylor expansion,

\left\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\Re(\varpi{\mathbf{H}}(p)))\right\rangle=\int\tfrac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}}{\hbar}f_{1}(h^{\prime}_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}^{\prime})\cdot k\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{1}){\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v\\
+\int_{0}^{1}\int{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}f_{1}((1-s)h_{2}\nabla^{2}g(v_{1}+s\hbar k)+sg_{1}\nabla^{2}h(v_{2}-\hbar k)):k^{\otimes 2}\\
\times\Re\big(\varpi(k,v_{1}){\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)(v_{1})\big)\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s,

Using Lemma
5.5
to treat the first and Proposition
5.3
to treat the second term, we obtain the expected estimation.
∎

The following estimation provides the regularity gain provided by the dissipation.

Proposition 5.11
.

Fix
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{2}(\mathbb{R})
such that
\inf_{k,v_{1}}\tfrac{1}{\varpi(k,v_{1})}\geq\frac{1}{C_{0}}
for some
C_{0}>1
.

There exists a constant
C_{\Phi_{0}}>1
depending only on
\Phi_{0}
such that for any measurable functions
f,g
, for any
K>1
,

(5.46)

\left\langle Q_{\hbar}(f,g,\varpi),f\right\rangle+\frac{1}{C_{0}}A_{\hbar}(f)\\
\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\left(A_{\hbar}(f)\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{\tfrac{d}{2}+4}(\Phi_{0}-g)\|_{H^{2}}+K^{-1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tfrac{d}{2}+4}g\|_{H^{2}}\right)+K^{4}\|f\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\langle v\rangle^{\tfrac{d}{2}+4}g\|_{H^{2}}\right).

Proof.

Using the change of variable
(v_{1},v_{2},k)\mapsto(v_{1}^{\prime},v_{2}^{\prime},-k)

\displaystyle\big\langle Q_{\hbar}(f,g,\varpi),f\big\rangle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int f_{1}(g^{\prime}_{2}f^{\prime}_{1}-g_{2}f_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int(f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1})g_{2}f_{1}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\hbar^{2}}\int(f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1})^{2}g_{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int((f^{\prime}_{1})^{2}-(f_{1})^{2})g_{2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

The last term is bounded by Proposition
5.7
.

Using Proposition
5.2
, there exists a constant
C>1
such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int(f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1})^{2}g_{2}

\displaystyle{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int(f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1})^{2}\Phi_{0}(v_{2}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int(f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1})^{2}\left|\Phi_{0}(v_{2})-g(v_{2})\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{C_{0}}A_{\hbar}(f)+C\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}(A_{\hbar}(f)+\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}})\|\langle v\rangle^{\tfrac{d}{2}+4}(g-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{2}}.

This conclude the proof.
∎

Proposition 5.12
.

Consider
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{1}(\mathbb{R})
, and
f,g,h\in L^{2}\mathbb{R}^{d}
. We have

(5.47)

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}g,h,\varpi)-\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi)\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|_{H^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{L^{2}},

(5.48)

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}g,h,\varpi)-\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi)\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m-1}g)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{m-1}g\|_{L^{2}})\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{1}}.

Consider
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{1}(\mathbb{C})
, and
f,g,h,p\in L^{2}\mathbb{R}^{d}
. We have for any
K>1

(5.49)

\left|\left\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}g,h,\Re(\varpi{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)))-\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\Re(\varpi{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)))\right\rangle\right|\\
\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}}\Big(K^{4}\|f\|_{L^{2}}+K^{-1}A_{\hbar}(f)\Big)\|\langle v\rangle^{m-1}g\|_{H^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{2}}\left(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Proof.

Perform the decomposition

\left\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}g,h,\varpi)-\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi)\right\rangle=\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\int f^{\prime}_{1}g_{1}h_{2}(\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}-\langle v_{1}^{\prime}\rangle^{m}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V\\
=J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}

where

(5.50)

\displaystyle J_{1}:=\frac{m}{\hbar}\int f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}h_{2}\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v^{\prime}_{1}\cdot k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

(5.51)

\displaystyle J_{2}:=\frac{m}{\hbar}\int f^{\prime}_{1}h_{2}(g_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v_{1}-g^{\prime}_{1}\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v^{\prime}_{1})\cdot k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

(5.52)

\displaystyle J_{3}:=\int_{0}^{1}(1-s)\int f^{\prime}_{1}g_{1}h_{2}W_{m}(v_{1}+s\hbar k):k^{\otimes 2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

where
W_{m}(v):=m\langle v\rangle^{m-4}(\langle v\rangle^{2}+(m-2)v^{\otimes 2})
. Using that
|\hbar k|\leq|v_{1}-v_{2}|\leq|v_{1}|+|v_{2}|
,

|W_{m}(v_{1}+s\hbar k)|\lesssim\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m-2}+\langle\hbar k\rangle^{m-2}\lesssim\langle\hbar k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}-2}(\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m-2}+\langle v_{2}\rangle^{m-\frac{d}{2}}).

We deduce that

|J_{3}|\lesssim\int\left|f^{\prime}_{1}\right|\left|\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m-2}g_{1}\right|\left|\langle v_{2}\rangle^{m-\frac{d}{2}}h_{2}\right|\frac{|\varpi(k,v_{1})|}{\langle k\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}d+3+\eta}}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

Step
1.
Proof of (
5.47
) and (
5.48
). Applying Proposition
5.3
,

\displaystyle J_{2}\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}_{0}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\min((A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m-1}g)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{m-1}g\|_{L^{2}})\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{1}},\|\langle v\rangle^{m-1}g\|_{H_{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{L^{2}}),

\displaystyle J_{3}\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\min\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{m}g\|_{H^{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}h\|_{L^{2}},\|\langle v\rangle^{m}g\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}h\|_{H^{1}}\right).

Using the symmetry
(v_{1},v_{2},k)\mapsto(v_{1}^{\prime},v_{2}^{\prime},-k)
,

\displaystyle J_{1}=\frac{m}{2\hbar^{2}}\int(h_{2}f^{\prime}_{1}g^{\prime}_{1}\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v^{\prime}_{1}-h^{\prime}_{2}f_{1}g_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v^{\prime}_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

Hence,

\displaystyle|J_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\int\left|\tfrac{h_{2}-h_{2}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\right|\left|f^{\prime}_{1}\right|\left|g^{\prime}_{1}\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{m-1}\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\int\left|h^{\prime}_{2}\right|\left|\tfrac{f^{\prime}_{1}-f_{1}}{\hbar}\right|\left|g^{\prime}_{1}\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{m-1}\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\int\left|h^{\prime}_{2}\right|\left|f^{\prime}_{1}\right|\left|\tfrac{g_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v_{1}-g^{\prime}_{1}\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v_{1}^{\prime}}{\hbar}\right|{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1}-\hbar k)}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

We can conclude by applying Proposition
5.3
.

Step
2.
For
\tilde{\varpi}\in\mathscr{B}^{2}(\mathbb{C})
and
p\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, we denote
\varpi:=\Re(\tilde{\varpi}{\mathbf{H}}(p))
. We can perform the same decomposition

\displaystyle\Big\langle f,Q_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}g,h,\Re(\tilde{\varpi}{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p)))-\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\Re(\tilde{\varpi}{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(p))\Big\rangle=J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}

Performing a Taylor expansion of
(g_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v_{1}-g^{\prime}_{1}\langle v^{\prime}_{1}\rangle^{m-2}v^{\prime}_{1})
and applying Proposition
5.3
,

|J_{2}+J_{3}|\leq\|\tilde{\varpi}\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}g\|_{H^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}h\|_{H^{2}}(A_{\hbar}(p)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|p\|_{L^{2}}).

Using Proposition
5.5
, for any
K>1

J_{1}\lesssim\|\tilde{\varpi}\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\Big(K^{4}\|f\|_{L^{2}}+K^{-1}A_{\hbar}(f)^{\tfrac{1}{2}}\Big)\|\langle v\rangle^{m-1}g\|_{H^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}h\|_{H^{2}}\left(A_{\hbar}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}(p)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}p\|_{L^{2}}\right).

This conclude the proof. ∎

In order to obtain a short time existence (via a Picard fix point argument), we need the following Lipschitz stability estimates on the collision operator:

Proposition 5.13
.

Fix
N\geq 4
,
m\geq\frac{d}{2}+4
, and
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}
such that
|\sum_{i}\alpha_{i}|\leq N
. Fix
\Phi_{0}
such that
\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\Phi_{0}\|_{H^{N}}
and
\|\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi_{0}|)}\|_{L^{\infty}}
are bounded. There exists a constant
C_{\Phi_{0}}>0
(depending on
\Phi_{0}
) such that for any
f,g,h
,

(5.53)

\left\langle f,\langle v\rangle^{m}\left(Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{3}}_{v}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g)|^{2}})-Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}h,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}h,\partial^{\alpha_{3}}_{v}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(h)|^{2}})\right)\right\rangle\\
\leq\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\max_{F\in\{g,h\}}\left(\frac{C_{\Phi_{0}}}{\left(1-C_{\Phi_{0}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}(F-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{N}}\right)^{2N+2}}\right)\|f\|_{H_{N}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(g-h)\|_{H_{N}}.

Proof.

We recall the Faà di Bruno formula,

\displaystyle\nabla^{|\alpha_{3}|}_{v_{1}}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g,k,v_{1})|^{2}}=\sum_{k,l=0}^{|\alpha_{3}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma_{1}+\cdots+\gamma_{k}\\
+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}+\cdots+\tilde{\gamma}_{l}=|\alpha_{3}|\\
\gamma_{i},\tilde{\gamma_{i}}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{(-1)^{k+l}r_{1}!k!l!}{(\gamma_{1}+\cdots\gamma_{k})!(\tilde{\gamma}_{1}+\cdots+\tilde{\gamma}_{l})!}\frac{\prod_{j=1}^{k}\nabla_{v_{1}}^{\gamma_{j}}\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g,k,v_{1})\prod_{j=1}^{l}\nabla_{v_{1}}^{\tilde{\gamma}_{j}}\overline{\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g,k,v_{1})}}{\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g,k,v_{1})^{k+1}\overline{\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g,k,v_{1})}^{l+1}}

As
\nabla^{\ell}_{v_{1}}\varepsilon(g,k,v_{1})={\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k){\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}\left(\nabla^{\ell}g\right)(v_{1})
, there exists three functions
(\varpi^{\alpha_{3}}_{\ell}(g))_{-1\leq\ell\leq 1}
depending on
{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)
and
({\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(\nabla^{r}g))_{r\leq N-2}

\partial_{v_{1}}^{\alpha_{3}}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g,k,v_{1})|^{2}}=\hat{k}^{\otimes\alpha_{3}}:\nabla^{|\alpha_{3}|}_{v_{1}}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g,k,v_{1})|^{2}}=:\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}(g,k,v_{1})+\sum_{\ell=0}^{1}\Re\left(\varpi^{\alpha_{3}}_{\ell}(g,k,v_{1}){\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(\nabla^{N-\ell}g)\right).

One can easily check that
\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}(g)
and
(\varpi^{\alpha_{3}}_{\ell}(g))_{\ell\leq 3}
verify the conditions (
5.1
) and
\varpi^{\alpha_{3}}_{\ell}(g)
vanishes if
|\alpha_{3}|<N-\ell
. In addition, as
\tilde{\varpi}^{|\alpha_{3}|}(g)
depends only on
({\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(\nabla^{r}g))_{r\leq N-2}
, using a variation of the proof of Proposition
3.1
we have

(5.54)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}(g)\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\leq C_{\Phi_{0}}\frac{1+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}g\|^{|\alpha_{3}|}_{H^{N}}}{\left(1-C_{\Phi_{0}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}(g-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{2}}\right)^{2|\alpha_{3}|+2}}

(5.55)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}(g)-\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}_{j}(h)\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\leq\max_{F\in\{g,h\}}\left(\frac{C_{\Phi_{0}}\left(1+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}F\|^{|\alpha_{3}|}_{H^{N}}\right)}{\left(1-C_{\Phi_{0}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}(F-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{2}}\right)^{2|\alpha_{3}|+2}}\right)\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}(g-h)\|_{H^{N}}.

In the same way, as
\varpi_{\ell}(g)
only depends on the
({\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(\nabla^{r}g))_{r\leq 2}
, we have

(5.56)

\displaystyle\|\varpi^{\alpha_{3}}_{j}(g)\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\leq C_{\Phi_{0}}\frac{1+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}g\|^{|\alpha_{3}|}_{H^{4}}}{\left(1-C_{\Phi_{0}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}(g-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{2}}\right)^{2|\alpha_{3}|+2}}

(5.57)

\displaystyle\|\varpi^{\alpha_{3}}_{j}(g)-\varpi^{\alpha_{3}}_{j}(h)\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\leq\max_{F\in\{g,h\}}\left(\frac{C_{\Phi_{0}}\left(1+\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}F\|^{|\alpha_{3}|}_{H^{4}}\right)}{\left(1-C_{\Phi_{0}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}(F-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{2}}\right)^{2|\alpha_{3}|+2}}\right)\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}(g-h)\|_{H^{4}}.

We obtain the decomposition

(5.58)

Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{3}}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g)|^{2}})=Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}g,\tilde{\varpi}(g))+\sum_{\ell=0}^{1}Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}g,\Re(\varpi_{\ell}^{\alpha_{3}}(g){\mathbf{H}}(\nabla^{N-\ell}g)).

Using that
5.8
and that
Q_{\hbar}
is trilinear and

•

(
5.41
) and (
5.48
) if
\max(|\alpha_{1}|,|\alpha_{2}|)\leq N-1
(remark that
|\alpha_{1}|=|\alpha_{2}|=N-1
is impossible since
N\geq 4
),

•

(
5.40
) and (
5.48
) if
|\alpha_{1}|=N
(then
|\alpha_{2}|\leq N-2
and
|\alpha_{3}|\leq N-4
),

•

(
5.42
) and (
5.47
) if
|\alpha_{2}|=N
(then
|\alpha_{1}|\leq N-2
and
|\alpha_{3}|\leq N-4
),

we obtain

\left|\left\langle f,\langle v\rangle^{l}\left(Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}g,\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}(g))-Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}h,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}h,\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}(h))\right)\right\rangle\right|\\
\leq\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\max_{F\in\{g,h\}}\left(\frac{C_{\Phi_{0}}}{\left(1-C_{\Phi_{0}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}(F-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{N}}\right)^{2N+2}}\right)\|\langle v\rangle^{l}f\|_{H_{N}}\|\langle v\rangle^{l}(g-h)\|_{H_{N}}.

If
\varpi_{\ell}(g)
is non-zero, one has
|\alpha_{3}|\geq N-1
. We deduce that
|\alpha_{1}|\leq 1\leq N-3
and
|\alpha_{2}|\leq 1\leq N-3
. Hence, one can apply (
5.43
) and (
5.49
) and obtain

\sum_{\ell=0}^{1}\left|\left\langle f,\langle v\rangle^{l}\left(Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}g,\Re\left(\varpi_{\ell}^{\alpha_{3}}(g){\mathbf{H}}(\nabla^{N-\ell}g)\right))-Q_{\hbar}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}h,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}h,\Re\left(\varpi_{\ell}^{\alpha_{3}}(h){\mathbf{H}}(\nabla^{N-\ell}h)\right)\right)\right\rangle\right|\\
\leq\frac{1}{\hbar^{2}}\max_{F\in\{g,h\}}\left(\frac{C_{\Phi_{0}}}{\left(1-C_{\Phi_{0}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}(F-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{N}}\right)^{2N+2}}\right)\|\langle v\rangle^{l}f\|_{H_{N}}\|\langle v\rangle^{l}(g-h)\|_{H_{N}}.

This concludes the proof.
∎

5.4.
Cauchy theory for non-pertubative solution of the quantum Lenard-Balescu equation

The goal of the present section is to prove Theorem
3
. We split the proof into two steps: first we prove a local existence result, and in a a second time, we will provide some energy estimation.

Proposition 5.14
.

Fix
N\geq 4
,
m\geq\frac{d}{2}+4
. Fix
\Phi_{0}
such that
\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\Phi_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}
and
\|\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{0}(\Phi_{0})|}\|_{L^{\infty}}
are bounded. There exist two constants
T>0
and
\delta>0
depending on
\Phi_{0}
but independent from
\hbar
such that for any
\varphi_{0}
with
\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\varphi_{0}\|_{H^{N}}<\delta
, there exists a unique solution
\Phi_{0}+\varphi
of the equation (
QLB
) with initial data
\Phi_{0}+\varphi_{0}
. In addition,
\langle v\rangle^{m}\varphi\in\mathscr{C}([0,\tfrac{T}{\hbar^{2}}],H^{N})
.

Proof.

First, as
\|\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon_{0}(\Phi_{0})}\|_{L^{\infty}}
and
\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\Phi_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}
are bounded, from Proposition
3.1
, for some positive constant
C>0

{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi_{0},k,v_{1})|}\geq\left\|\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{0}(\Phi_{0}|)}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}^{-1}-C\hbar\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\Phi_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}},

which is bigger than
\|\tfrac{1}{2\varepsilon_{0}(\Phi_{0})}\|_{L^{\infty}}
for
\hbar
small enough.

We recall the Quantum Lenard-Balescu equation

\partial_{t}\varphi(t)=Q_{\hbar}\left(\varphi(t),\varphi(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\right),\penalty\ \penalty\ \varphi(t=0)=\Phi_{0}+\varphi_{0}.

We recall the constant
C_{\Phi_{0}}
defined in Proposition
5.13
. We set

T:=\frac{1}{3^{N}2^{2N+3}C_{\Phi_{0}}},\penalty\ \delta:=\frac{1}{4C_{\Phi_{0}}}.

We will use a Picard fix-point argument: defining
\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}g\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}=\sup_{t\in[0,\frac{T}{\hbar^{2}}]}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}g(t,v)\|_{H_{N}(\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v)}
, it is sufficient to prove that the application

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}:g\mapsto\left[(t,v)\mapsto\Phi_{0}+\varphi_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}Q_{\hbar}\left(g(s),g(s),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(g(s))|^{2}}\right)\right]

is stable and contractant on

\mathscr{E}:=\left\{g|\langle v\rangle^{m}g\in\mathscr{C}([0,\tfrac{T}{\hbar^{2}}],H^{N}),\penalty\ g(0,v)=\Phi_{0}+\varphi_{0},\penalty\ \operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}g-\Phi_{0}\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}\leq 2\delta\right\}.

We only show the continuity estimates (stability is performed in the same way): for
g,h\in\mathscr{E}
, for all
t\in[0,\tfrac{T}{\hbar^{2}}]
,
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}^{d}
with
|\alpha|\leq N

\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\mathscr{F}g(t)-\mathscr{F}h(t))\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
=\int_{0}^{t}\Big\langle\langle v\rangle^{l}\partial^{\alpha}(\mathscr{F}g(t)-\mathscr{F}h(t)),\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}\Big(Q_{\hbar}\left(\Phi_{0}+g(s),\Phi_{0}+g(s),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi_{0}+g(s))|^{2}}\right)\\
-Q_{\hbar}\left(\Phi_{0}+h(s),\Phi_{0}+h(s),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\Phi_{0}+h(s))|^{2}}\right)\Big)\Big\rangle\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s.

Using that

\partial_{v}^{\alpha}Q_{\hbar}(f,g,\varpi)=\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}=\alpha}\binom{\alpha}{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}}Q_{\hbar}(\partial_{v}^{\alpha_{1}}f,\partial_{v}^{\alpha_{2}}g,\partial_{v}^{\alpha_{3}}\varpi),

and (
5.53
), we obtain that

\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\mathscr{F}g(t)-\mathscr{F}h(t))\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{3^{N}2^{2N+2}C_{\Phi_{0}}}{\hbar^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{l}\partial^{\alpha}(\mathscr{F}g(t)-\mathscr{F}h(t))\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(g(s)-h(s))\|_{H^{N}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}s

and finally, passing to the supremum in
t
and
\alpha
,

\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}\mathscr{F}g-\mathscr{F}h\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}\leq T3^{N}2^{2N+2}C_{\Phi_{0}}\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}g-h\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}g-h\operatorname{|\hskip-1.13809pt|\hskip-1.13809pt|}.

∎

We prove now that the validity time does not depend on
\hbar
. We only need a long time energy estimate:

Proposition 5.15
.

Fix
N\geq 4
,
m\geq\frac{d}{2}+4
. Fix
\Phi_{0}
such that
\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\Phi_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}
and
\|\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{0}(\Phi_{0})}\|_{L^{\infty}}
are bounded. There exist a constant
\tilde{T}>0
depending on
\Phi_{0}
but independent from
\hbar
such that any solution
\Phi_{0}+\varphi(t)
of (
QLB
) with initial data
\Phi_{0}
satisfies

\sup_{t\in[0,\tilde{T}]}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{N}}\leq\delta,

where
\delta
is defined in Proposition
5.14
.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, one can suppose that
\delta<1
. We will show that

\tilde{T}:=\sup\left\{t>0,\penalty\ \forall s\leq t,\penalty\ \|\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{N}}\leq\delta\leq 1\right\}.

We recall the Equation (
QLB
):

\partial_{t}\varphi(t)=Q_{\hbar}\left(\varphi(t),\varphi(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\right),\penalty\ \varphi(t=0)=\Phi_{0}.

We deduce that for
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}^{d}
with
|\alpha|\leq N
,

\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}t}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
=\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}=\alpha}\binom{\alpha}{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}}\left\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\varphi(t),\partial^{\alpha_{2}}\varphi(t),\partial^{\alpha_{3}}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\right)\right\rangle.

Using the same decomposition of
Q_{\hbar}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\varphi(t),\partial^{\alpha_{2}}\varphi(t),\partial^{\alpha_{3}}\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\right)
defined in (
5.58
), we obtain

\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}t}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\binom{\alpha}{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}}\bigg[\left\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\varphi(t),\partial^{\alpha_{2}}\varphi(t),\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}(\varphi(t))\right)\right\rangle\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\in\{0,1\}\\
|\alpha_{3}|\geq N-\ell-1\end{subarray}}\left\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}\varphi(t),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}),\partial^{\alpha_{2}}\varphi(t),\Re\left[\varpi^{\alpha_{3}}_{\ell}(\varphi(t)){\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(\nabla^{N-\ell}\varphi(t))\right]\right)\right\rangle\bigg].

We split the set of the
(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3},\ell)
into four peaces,

We begin by the case
\alpha_{1}=\alpha
.

Note that
\tilde{\varpi}^{0}:=\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}
. Using Propositions
3.1
and
5.11
, there exists a constant
C_{\Phi_{0}}
depending only on
\Phi_{0}
(we recall that
\delta
only depend on
\Phi_{0}
) such that
\forall t\in[0,\tilde{T}[
and
K>1

\displaystyle\Big\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)

\displaystyle-\Phi_{0}),Q_{\hbar}\Big(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}),\varphi(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\Big)\Big\rangle+\frac{1}{C_{\Phi_{0}}}A_{\hbar}(f)

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{\hbar}\left(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\right)\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{\tfrac{d}{2}+4}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|_{H^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{K}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tfrac{d}{2}+4}\varphi(t)\|_{H^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+K^{4}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\tfrac{d}{2}+4}\varphi(t)\|_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))\left(\delta+\tfrac{1}{K}\right)+K^{4}.

Applying (
5.41
), we get
\forall t\in[0,\tilde{T}[
,
K>1

\displaystyle\Big\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}),Q_{\hbar}\Big(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}\Phi_{0},\varphi(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\Big)\Big\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}\Phi_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}\Phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\langle v\rangle^{\tfrac{d}{2}+4}\varphi(t)\|_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tfrac{1}{K}A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))+K.

Using (
5.48
), one has
\forall t\in[0,\tilde{T}[
,
K>1

\displaystyle\bigg\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}

\displaystyle(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}\Big(\partial^{\alpha}\varphi(t),\varphi(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\Big)-Q_{\hbar}\Big(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}\varphi(t),\varphi(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\Big)\bigg\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|_{L^{2}}\Big(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m-1}\partial^{\alpha}\varphi(t))^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1}\partial^{\alpha}\varphi(t)\|_{L^{2}}\Big)\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}\varphi(t)\|_{H^{1}},

\displaystyle\lesssim\tfrac{1}{K}A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))+K.

Finally,
\forall t\in[0,\tilde{T}[
,

\Big\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}\Big(\partial^{\alpha}\varphi(t),\varphi(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi(t))|^{2}}\Big)\Big\rangle+\frac{1}{C_{\Phi_{0}}}A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))\\
\lesssim A_{\hbar}\left(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\right)\left(\delta+\tfrac{1}{K}\right)+K^{4}.

In the same, using

•

estimations (
5.42
) and (
5.47
) for
\alpha_{2}=\alpha
,

•

(
5.41
) and (
5.48
) if
\max(|\alpha_{1}|,|\alpha_{2}|)\leq N-1
(remark that
|\alpha_{1}|=|\alpha_{2}|=N-1
is impossible since
N\geq 4
),

we obtain for any
K>1

\Big\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}\Big(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\varphi(t),\partial^{\alpha_{2}}\varphi(t),\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}(\varphi(t))\Big)\Big\rangle\\
\lesssim\tfrac{1}{K}A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))+A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))^{\frac{1}{2}}+K^{4}.

If
\tilde{\varpi}^{\alpha_{3}}_{\ell}
is non-zero, one has
|\alpha_{3}|\geq N-1
. We deduce that
|\alpha_{1}|\leq 1\leq N-3
and
|\alpha_{2}|\leq 1\leq N-3
. Hence, one can apply (
5.43
) and (
5.49
) and obtain

\left|\left\langle\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0},\langle v\rangle^{l}Q_{\hbar}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\varphi(t),\partial^{\alpha_{2}}\varphi(t),\Re\left[\varpi_{\ell}^{\alpha_{3}}(\varphi(t)){\mathbf{H}}(\nabla^{N-\ell}\varphi(t))\right]\right)\right\rangle\right|\\
\lesssim(\tfrac{1}{K}+\delta)A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))^{\frac{1}{2}}+K^{4}.

Summing on
(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3},\ell)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}t}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\tfrac{1}{C_{\Phi_{0}}}A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0}))

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{\hbar}\left(\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\right)\left(\delta+\tfrac{1}{K}\right)+K^{4}.

for
K>1
,
\delta<1
. Choosing
K,\tfrac{1}{\delta}
large enough , we deduce that for any
t\in[0,\tilde{T}[

(5.59)

\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\partial^{\alpha}(\varphi(t)-\Phi_{0})\|_{L^{2}}\leq\tilde{C}_{\Phi_{0}}\sqrt{t}

for some large constant
\tilde{C}_{\Phi_{0}}
depending on
\Phi_{0}
. This concludes the proof.
∎

5.5.
Semi-classical (or grazing collision) limit for non-perturbative solution

For
g,h
two functions
\mathbb{R}_{v}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
,
\varpi\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{0}(\mathbb{R})
(with
\hbar\geq 0
), we define

(5.60)

Q_{0}(f,g,\varpi)(v_{1}):={\rm div}_{v_{1}}\int k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})k\cdot\left(\nabla f_{1}g_{2}-f_{1}\nabla g_{2}\right)\delta_{k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1})}\hskip 1.0pt{\rm{}^{-}\!\!\!\!d}k\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v_{2}.

The classical Lenard-Balescu equation can be written as (we linearize around the initial data)

(5.61)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi_{0}=Q_{0}\left(\varphi_{0}(t),\varphi_{0}(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{0}(\varphi_{0}(t))|^{2}}\right),\varphi_{0}(t=0)=\Phi_{0}.

Proposition 5.16
.

For any
g,h\in H^{3}(\langle v\rangle^{d+8}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v)
,
\varpi_{\hbar}\in\mathscr{B}_{\hbar}^{3}(\mathbb{R})
and
\varpi_{0}\in\mathscr{B}_{0}^{3}(\mathbb{R})
. Then

(5.62)

\left|\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi_{\hbar})-\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{0}(g,h,\varpi_{0})\right|\leq\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}g\|_{H^{3}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}h\|_{H^{3}}(\hbar\|\varpi_{\hbar}\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}+\|\varpi_{\hbar}-\varpi_{0}\|_{\mathscr{B}^{1}})

Proof.

Performing the change of variable
v_{2}\mapsto v_{2}^{\prime}
and a Taylor expension, for any
f\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, (denoting
v_{2}^{\prime\prime}:=v_{2}+\hbar k
),

\displaystyle\big\langle f,\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(g,h,\varpi_{\hbar})\big\rangle=\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\tfrac{g_{1}^{\prime}h_{2}-g_{1}h_{2}^{\prime\prime}}{\hbar^{2}}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\Big(\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}}{\hbar}\cdot k+\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla^{2}g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla^{2}h_{2}}{2}:k^{\otimes 2}\Big){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi_{\hbar}(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V+\hbar\mathfrak{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\Big(\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}}{2}\cdot k(\varpi_{\hbar}(k,v_{1})-\underset{=\varpi_{\hbar}(k,v_{1}-\hbar k)}{\varpi_{\hbar}(-k,v_{1}))}+\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla^{2}g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla^{2}h_{2}}{2}:k^{\otimes 2}\varpi_{\hbar}(k,v_{1})\Big){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V+\hbar\mathfrak{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\Big(\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}}{2}\otimes\nabla_{v_{1}}\varpi_{\hbar}(k,v_{1})+\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla^{2}g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla^{2}h_{2}}{2}\varpi(k,v_{1})\Big):k^{\otimes 2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V+\hbar\big(\mathfrak{R}_{1}+\mathfrak{R}_{2}\big),

where, denoting
(v_{1,s},v_{2,s}):=(v_{1}+s\hbar k,v_{2}-s\hbar k)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{1}:=\int_{0}^{1}\tfrac{(1-s)^{2}}{2}\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla^{3}g(v_{1,s})-g_{1}\nabla^{3}h(v_{2,s})}{2}:k^{\otimes 3}\varpi_{\hbar}(k,v_{1}){\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{2}:=\int_{0}^{1}(1-s)\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla g(v_{1})-g_{1}\nabla h(v_{2})}{2}\otimes\nabla^{2}\varpi_{\hbar}(k,v_{1}-s\hbar k):k^{\otimes 3}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V.

Using that
k\cdot(v_{2}-v_{1,s}-s\hbar k)=0
, one has
|s\hbar k|\leq|v_{1,s}-v_{2}|
and
\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\leq\langle v_{1,s}\rangle^{m}+\langle s\hbar k\rangle^{m}\leq\langle s\hbar k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}}(\langle v_{1,s}\rangle^{m}+\langle v_{2}\rangle^{m-\frac{d}{2}})
. Hence,

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{R}_{1}|\lesssim\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{0}}\int_{0}^{1}\int|f_{1}|\Big(\langle v_{2}\rangle^{m-\frac{d}{2}}|h_{2}||\langle v_{1,s}\rangle^{m}\nabla^{3}g(v_{1,s})|+\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}|g_{1}||\nabla^{3}h(v_{2,s})|\langle k\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+3}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

Applying Proposition
5.3
,

|\mathfrak{R}_{1}|+|\mathfrak{R}_{2}|\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\varpi\|_{\mathscr{B}^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}g\|_{H^{3}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{d}{2}+4}\|_{H^{3}}.

On the other hand, using that
(\nabla_{v_{1}}+\nabla_{v_{2}})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}+v_{2})}=0
,

\displaystyle\big\langle f,

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{0}(g,h,\varpi_{0})\big\rangle=\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\nabla_{1}\cdot\big(k\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}}{2}\cdot k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi_{0}(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\big)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}(\nabla_{1}+\nabla_{2})\cdot\big(k\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}}{2}\cdot k{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\varpi_{0}(k,v_{1})\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\big)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=\int f_{1}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{m}\big(\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}}{2}\otimes\nabla_{v_{1}}\varpi_{0}(k,v_{1})+\tfrac{h_{2}\nabla g_{1}-g_{1}\nabla h_{2}}{2}\varpi_{0}(k,v_{1})\big):k^{\otimes 2}{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}\delta_{k\cdot(v_{1}-v_{2})}\big)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}V

Applying again Proposition
5.3
, we can conclude.
∎

We can finally prove the theorem.

Proof.

We fix
\Phi_{0}\in H^{N+1}(\langle v\rangle^{2m}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v)
and
\|\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon_{0}(\Phi_{0})}\|
are bounded. Thanks to Theorem
3
, there exists
T>0
and
\hbar_{0}
such that for any
\hbar<\hbar_{0}
, there exist
\varphi_{\hbar}(t)\in\mathscr{C}_{b}^{0}(H^{N}(\langle v\rangle^{2m}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v))
mild solution of (
QLB
), with
\sup_{\hbar\in]0,\hbar_{0}[}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|g_{\hbar}(t)\|_{r}^{2}
bounded. In
[
DW23
]
, the authors show the existence of
\varphi_{\infty}\in\mathscr{C}_{b}^{0}(H^{N}(\langle v\rangle^{2m}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v))
mild solution of (
CLB
) with initial data
\varphi_{\infty}(t=0):=g_{0}
.

We perform again an energy estimation:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}}\|

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t))\|^{2}=\big\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-\varphi_{\infty}(t),\varphi_{\hbar}(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t))|^{2}})\big\rangle

\displaystyle+\big\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\infty}(t),\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-\varphi_{\infty}(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t))|^{2}})\big\rangle

\displaystyle+\big\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)),\langle v\rangle^{m}Q_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\infty}(t),\varphi_{\infty}(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t))|^{2}}-\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\infty}(t))|^{2}})\big\rangle

\displaystyle+\big\langle\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)),\langle v\rangle^{m}\big(Q_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\infty}(t),\varphi_{\infty}(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\infty}(t))|^{2}})-Q_{0}(\varphi_{\infty}(t),\varphi_{\infty}(t),\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{0}(\varphi_{\infty}(t))|^{2}})\big)\big\rangle.

The first line is bounded by (
5.46
) and (
5.48
), the second line by (
5.42
) and (
5.48
), and the fourth by (
5.62
). In order to treat the third line, we remark that

\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t))|^{2}}-\tfrac{1}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\infty}(t))|^{2}}=-\Re\left[\tfrac{{\hat{\mathscr{V}}}(k)^{2}(\overline{\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t))}+\overline{\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\infty}(t)))}}{|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)|^{2}|\varepsilon_{\hbar}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)|^{2}}{\mathbf{H}}_{\hbar k}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-\varphi_{\infty}(t))\right],

thus one can apply (
5.43
) and (
5.49
). Finally, as
\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\varphi_{\hbar}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T],H^{N})}
and
\|\langle v\rangle^{m}\varphi_{\hbar}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T],H^{N})}
are both bounded, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t))\|^{2}+A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)))\\
\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t))\|^{2}+\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t))\|\left(A_{\hbar}(\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t)))^{\frac{1}{2}}+\hbar\right)

Applying Young inequality,

\displaystyle\frac{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}}{\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}{t}}\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t))\|^{2}\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t))\|^{2}+\hbar^{2}

and by Gronwall inequality, there exists a constant
C
such that for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(5.63)

\|\langle v\rangle^{m}(\varphi_{\hbar}(t)-g_{\infty}(t))\|^{2}\leq\hbar^{2}C(e^{Ct}-1).

This concludes the proof.
∎

Appendix A
Bound on some convolution operators

The goal of this section is to provide a proof of Lemma
5.6
:

Lemma A.1
.

Fix
k\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Denoting
L_{\perp}^{p}(L_{\shortparallel}^{q}):=L^{p}(<k>^{\perp},L^{q}(\mathbb{R}\hat{k}))
. There exists a constant
C>0
such that for any function
f
and any
K>1
, for any
d\geq 2
, one has

(A.1)

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot v+i0}\ast f\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\lesssim\ \left\|f\right\|_{L^{1}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})},\penalty\ \big\|\tfrac{1-\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot v+i0}\ast f\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\lesssim K^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}},

for any
d\geq 3
, for any
p\in[1,\tfrac{d-1}{d-2}]
and
q=\tfrac{p(d-1)}{d-1-(d-2)p}
,

(A.2)

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}+\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)v}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}+\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)|v^{\perp}|}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\leq C\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})},

for any
d\geq 4
, for any
p\in[1,\tfrac{d-1}{d-3}]
and
q=\tfrac{p(d-1)}{d-1-(d-3)p}
,

(A.3)

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\leq C\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})}

for
d=2
and for any
p,q\in[1,\infty]
with
p>q

(A.4)

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}+\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)v}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\leq CK^{\tfrac{1}{q}-\tfrac{1}{p}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})},

(A.5)

\displaystyle\big\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{(\hat{k}\cdot v+i0)|v|^{2}}\ast f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\leq CK^{1+\tfrac{1}{q}-\tfrac{1}{p}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{p}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{{\shortparallel}})}

Proof.

We define in the following

\displaystyle\varphi_{1}(v):=\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{v^{\shortparallel}+i0},\penalty\ \varphi_{2}(v):=\tfrac{1-\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{v^{\shortparallel}+i0},\penalty\ \varphi_{3}(v):=\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{|v^{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|v^{\perp}|^{2}},

\displaystyle\varphi_{4}(v):=\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)v^{\perp}}{(v^{\shortparallel}+i0)(|v^{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|v^{\perp}|^{2})},\penalty\ \varphi_{5}(v):=\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)|v^{\perp}|}{(v^{\shortparallel}+i0)(|v^{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|v^{\perp}|^{2})},\penalty\ \varphi_{6}(v):=\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{(v^{\shortparallel}+i0)(|v^{\shortparallel}|^{2}+|v^{\perp}|^{2})}.

We denote
\check{\varphi}(\xi,v^{\perp}):=\int e^{i\xi v^{\shortparallel}}\varphi(v^{\shortparallel},v^{\perp})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v^{\shortparallel}
the Fourier transform with respect to the first variable. We recall that, denoting
\operatorname{sg}(\xi):=(1-2\mathbbm{1}_{\xi<0})
, the Fourier transform of
\tfrac{1}{v^{\shortparallel}+i0}
is
-2i\pi\mathbbm{1}_{\xi>0}
, the Fourier transform of
\tfrac{1}{1+|v^{\shortparallel}|^{2}}
is
\pi\exp(-|\xi|)
and the Fourier transform of
\tfrac{1}{(v^{\shortparallel}+i0)(1+|v^{\shortparallel}|^{2})}
is
i\pi\operatorname{sg}(\xi)(1-\exp(-|\xi|))
. Hence,

\displaystyle|\check{\varphi}_{2}(\xi,v^{\perp})|\lesssim 1-\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)\text{ and }\forall i\in\{1,3,4,5,6\},\penalty\ |\check{\varphi}_{i}(\xi,v^{\perp})|\lesssim\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{|v^{\perp}|^{r_{i}}},

where
r_{1}=0
,
r_{3}=r_{4}=r_{5}=1
and
r_{6}=2
.

•

Proof of (
A.1
).
Using Parseval inequality, for any
v^{\perp}\in<k>^{\perp}
,

\int\big|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot v+i0}\ast f\big|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v^{\shortparallel}\leq 2\pi\int\bigg|\int\check{f}(\xi,u^{\perp})\chi(K|v^{\perp}-u^{\perp}|)\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}u^{\perp}\bigg|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\xi\leq 2\pi\bigg\|\int|\check{f}(\cdot,u^{\perp})|\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}u^{\perp}\bigg\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\\
\leq 2\pi\bigg(\int\|\check{f}(\cdot,u^{\perp})\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}u^{\perp}\bigg)^{2}\leq 2\pi\|\check{f}(\cdot,u^{\perp})\|_{L^{1}_{\perp}L^{2}_{\shortparallel}}^{2}.

In the same way,

\int\big|\tfrac{1-\chi(K|v^{\perp}|)}{\hat{k}\cdot v+i0}\ast f\big|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}v^{\shortparallel}\leq 2\pi\int\bigg|\int\check{f}(\xi,u^{\perp})(1-\chi(K|v^{\perp}-u^{\perp}|))\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}u^{\perp}\bigg|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\xi\\
\leq 2\pi\int(1-\chi(K|u^{\perp}|))^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}u^{\perp}\int|\check{f}(\xi,u^{\perp})|^{2}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}u^{\perp}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\xi\lesssim 2K^{d-1}\pi\|f\|_{L^{2}}.

•

Proof of (
A.2
) and (
A.3
).
Using Parseval inequality, for
i\in\{3,4,5\}

\big\|\varphi_{i}\ast f(v)\big\|_{L^{2}_{\shortparallel}}=\bigg\|\int\check{\varphi}_{i}(\xi,v^{\perp}-\tilde{v}^{\perp})\check{f}(\xi,\tilde{v}^{\perp})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}^{\perp}\bigg\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\bigg\|\int\tfrac{\check{f}(\xi,\tilde{v}^{\perp})}{|v^{\perp}-\tilde{v}^{\perp}|^{r_{i}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}^{\perp}\bigg\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\int\tfrac{\|{f}(\cdot,\tilde{v}^{\perp})\|_{L^{2}_{\shortparallel}}}{|v^{\perp}-\tilde{v}^{\perp}|^{r_{i}}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}^{\perp}.

Applying the Hardy-Littelwood inequality,
\big\|\varphi_{i}\ast_{v}f\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}\lesssim\big\|\check{f}\big\|_{L^{q}_{\perp}(L^{2}_{\shortparallel})}
if

\frac{r_{i}}{d-1}+\frac{1}{p}=1+\frac{1}{q}.

•

Proof of (
A.4
) and (
A.5
).
Using Parseval inequality, for
i\in\{3,4,5\}

\displaystyle\big\|\varphi_{i}\ast f(v)\big\|_{L^{2}_{\shortparallel}}=\bigg\|\int\check{\varphi}_{i}(\xi,v^{\perp}-\tilde{v}^{\perp})\check{f}(\xi,\tilde{v}^{\perp})\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}^{\perp}\bigg\|_{L^{2}(\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\xi)}\lesssim\int\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}-\tilde{v}^{\perp}|)}{|v^{\perp}-\tilde{v}^{\perp}|^{r_{i}}}\|{f}(\cdot,\tilde{v}^{\shortparallel})\|_{L^{2}_{\shortparallel}}\hskip 1.0pt\mathrm{d}\tilde{v}^{\perp}.

Using that for
\alpha>1
,
\|\tfrac{\chi(K|v^{\perp}|}{|v^{\perp}|^{r_{i}}}\|_{L^{\alpha}(<k>^{\perp})}=O(K^{r_{i}-1+\tfrac{1}{\alpha}})
and the Young’s inequality, we obtain the expected result.

∎
